\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left\{\frac{1}{2}\frac{k^{2}\partial_{t}p}{p^{3}}|\hat{N}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}+\left(\delta_{1}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}-2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}\right)|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}+\left[\frac{5}{2}\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}-\frac{\mu}{2}\frac{k^{2}\partial_{t}p}{p^{\frac{5}{2}}}\right.\right.

(5.40)

\displaystyle-\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\left(\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\frac{1}{8}\frac{\partial_{t}\varphi}{\varphi}+\frac{1}{4}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}+\frac{1}{4}\nu p\right)+2\delta_{1}\mu^{\frac{4}{3}}\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}

\displaystyle\left.+2\delta_{1}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\frac{1}{4}\frac{\partial_{t}\varphi}{\varphi}\right)+\delta_{1}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\nu p^{\frac{1}{2}}\right]Re\left(\hat{N}_{0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)

\displaystyle\left.+\left(2\delta_{1}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{k^{2}}{p^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}\frac{k^{2}\partial_{t}p}{p^{3}}\right)\varphi^{-\frac{1}{4}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)-2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\varphi^{-\frac{1}{4}}Re\left(\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right\}\,d\xi

\displaystyle+\delta_{1}\mu\sum_{s=1}^{3}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left\{|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{0,s}^{0}|^{2}+\nu|\xi|Re\left(\hat{N}_{0,s}^{0}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{0,s}^{0}\right)\right\}\,d\xi,

and the nonlinear terms
I_{i}(i=1,\cdots,5)
are defined by

\displaystyle I_{1}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,0,0}^{k}\bar{\hat{N}}_{0,0}^{k}\right)+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{N}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\,d\xi,

\displaystyle I_{2}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,0,0}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{0,0}^{k}\right)\,d\xi+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\,d\xi,

\displaystyle I_{3}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle-\delta_{1}\mu\sum_{s=1}^{3}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}|\xi|^{-1}\left[Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,0,s}^{0}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{0,s}^{0}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,0,s}^{0}\bar{\hat{N}}_{0,s}^{0}\right)\right]\,d\xi,

\displaystyle I_{4}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{4}\sum_{s=0}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\left[Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{N}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right]\,d\xi,

\displaystyle I_{5}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle-\delta_{1}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\sum_{s=0}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left[Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{N}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right]\,d\xi.

We now deal with all the terms on the right-hand side of (
5.39
).
First, we present the following lemma concerning the estimate of the linear term
{\mathcal{L}}_{1}
.

Lemma 5.1
.

It holds that

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{1}(t)\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{2}(t)+\frac{C}{A}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}(t).

(5.41)

Proof.

We begin by handling the linear terms with
k\not=0
on the right-hand side of (
5.40
).
Recalling the definitions of
\varphi
and
p
from (
3.1
) and (
4.5
), respectively, we immediately obtain

\displaystyle|\partial_{t}p|\leq 2|k|p^{\frac{1}{2}},\quad|\partial_{t}p|\leq p,\quad\left|\frac{\partial_{t}\varphi}{\varphi}\right|\leq\left|\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}\right|.

(5.42)

This implies that

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{2}\frac{k^{2}\partial_{t}p}{p^{3}}\right||\hat{N}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}\lesssim\frac{k^{2}}{p}|\hat{N}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}.

(5.43)

It is not difficult to check that

\displaystyle-2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}\leq 0.

(5.44)

With the help of (
5.42
) and Young’s inequality, we get

\displaystyle\left|-\frac{\nu}{4}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right|\lesssim\nu|k||Re\left(\hat{N}_{0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)|\leq\widetilde{\epsilon}\nu p|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}+C_{\widetilde{\epsilon}}\frac{k^{2}}{p}|\hat{N}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2},

(5.45)

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{2}\delta_{1}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\frac{\partial_{t}\varphi}{\varphi}Re\left(\hat{N}_{0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right|\lesssim\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\left|\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right|\leq\widetilde{\epsilon}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}+C_{\widetilde{\epsilon}}\frac{k^{2}}{p}|\hat{N}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}.

(5.46)

Again thanks to (
5.42
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\left(-\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}+2\delta_{1}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}\right)Re\left(\hat{N}_{0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right|

(5.47)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\left(\frac{1}{2}+2\delta_{1}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\right)\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}\right)\left|Re\left(\hat{N}_{0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{1}{2}+2\delta_{1}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\right)\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}\right)\left(|\hat{N}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}\right),

and

\displaystyle\left|\left[\frac{5}{2}\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}-\frac{\mu}{2}\frac{k^{2}\partial_{t}p}{p^{\frac{5}{2}}}-\frac{1}{4}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\left(\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}\varphi}{\varphi}+\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}\right)+2\delta_{1}\mu^{\frac{4}{3}}\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right]Re\left(\hat{N}_{0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right|

(5.48)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{k^{2}}{p}\left(|\hat{N}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}\right).

By virtue of (
5.42
) and Young’s inequality, we get

\displaystyle\left|\delta_{1}\mu^{\frac{4}{3}}p^{\frac{1}{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{UU}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right|\lesssim\delta_{1}\mu^{\frac{4}{3}}\left(|\hat{N}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}+p|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}\right).

(5.49)

The application of (
3.9
) and (
5.42
) yields

\displaystyle\left|\left(2\delta_{1}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{k^{2}}{p^{2}}-\frac{1}{2}\frac{k^{2}\partial_{t}p}{p^{3}}\right)\varphi^{-\frac{1}{4}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{1,s}^{k}\right)-2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\varphi^{-\frac{1}{4}}Re\left(\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{1,s}^{k}\right)\right|

(5.50)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{k^{2}}{p}\left(|\hat{N}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{W}_{1,s}^{k}|^{2}\right).

For
k=0
, it is easy to verify that

\displaystyle\left|\delta_{1}\mu\nu|\xi|Re\left(\hat{N}_{0,s}^{0}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{0,s}^{0}\right)\right|\lesssim\delta_{1}\mu^{2}\left(|\hat{N}_{0,s}^{0}|^{2}+|\xi|^{2}|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{0,s}^{0}|^{2}\right).

(5.51)

Collecting all estimates (
5.43
)-(
5.51
) above together, then choosing
A
defined in (
3.1
) suitably large,
\delta_{1}
and
\widetilde{\epsilon}
suitably small, we obtain (
5.41
) immediately.
We thereby complete the proof of this lemma.

∎

Furthermore, all the nonlinear terms
I_{i}(i=1,\cdots,5)
will be handled according to the following five lemmas.

Lemma 5.2
.

Under the assumptions of Proposition
5.3
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{1}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

(5.52)

Lemma 5.3
.

Under the assumptions of Proposition
5.3
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{2}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{1,2}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

(5.53)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Lemma 5.4
.

Under the assumptions of Proposition
5.3
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{3}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

(5.54)

Lemma 5.5
.

Under the assumptions of Proposition
5.3
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{4}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{1,2}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

(5.55)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Lemma 5.6
.

Under the assumptions of Proposition
5.3
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{5}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-\frac{2}{3}}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{1,2}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

(5.56)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

We shall assume Lemmas
5.2
–
5.6
for the moment and proceed with the proof of Proposition
5.3
; the proofs of these lemmas will be provided at the end of this section.

Proof of Proposition
5.3
.

Integrating identity (
5.39
) in time and inserting (
5.41
) together with (
5.52
)–(
5.56
) into the resulting expression, we obtain (
5.9
).
∎

5.2.1
Proof of Lemma
5.2

Remembering the definitions of
\mathcal{R}_{1}
and
{\mathscr{F}}_{1,0,s}(s=0,\cdots,3)
in (
3.12
) and (
5.31
), respectively, it is not difficult to deduce from (
2.11
) that

\displaystyle|I_{1}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}(\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}N){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N_{0,0}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\mathcal{R}_{1}\nabla^{s}(\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}N)N_{0,s}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

(5.57)

\displaystyle+\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}(ND){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N_{0,0}+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\mathcal{R}_{1}\nabla^{s}(ND)N_{0,s}\right)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle I_{11}+I_{12}+I_{13}.

We recall the definitions of
N_{0,s}
and
\mathcal{R}_{1}
in (
4.6
) and (
3.12
), respectively, to obtain that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}(\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}N)={\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\left(\bm{V}\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}N_{0,0}+[\mathcal{R}_{1},\bm{V}]\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}N\right)+\mathcal{R}_{1}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}V^{2}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}N\right).

(5.58)

Then, employing integration by parts, (
5.58
), and Lemma
4.1
, we find that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{11}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}}\left(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N_{0,0}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\right)\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N_{0,0}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

(5.59)

\displaystyle+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}V^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N_{0,0}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(\tau)\,d\tau.

In a same manner, we have

\displaystyle I_{12}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}|\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V}||N_{0,s}|^{2}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}[\mathcal{R}_{1},\bm{V}]\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}\nabla^{s}N\cdot N_{0,s}\,dxdy\right|

(5.60)

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\mathcal{R}_{1}([\nabla^{s},\bm{V}]\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}N)\cdot N_{0,s}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle I_{121}+I_{122}+I_{123}.

Notice that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V}={\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}V^{2}.

(5.61)

The application of Sobolev’s inequality, (
3.9
), (
5.61
), and Lemma
4.1
yields

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{121}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{3}\left(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}V^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}}\big(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}^{2}\big)\right.

(5.62)

\displaystyle\left.+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\infty}}\|N_{0,s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-\frac{5}{6}}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

While it is observe that

\displaystyle I_{122}=\sum_{s=1}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{0}\left([\mathcal{R}_{1},\bm{V}]\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}\nabla^{s}N\right)\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N_{0,s}+{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\left([\mathcal{R}_{1},\bm{V}]\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}\nabla^{s}N\right)\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N_{0,s}\right)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|.

(5.63)

We note that the decomposition in (
5.63
) will be used extensively throughout the proof.
Notice that

\displaystyle m_{1}=m_{2}=\varphi=1,\quad\text{for}~~k=0,

which, along with the definition of (
3.12
), gives

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\mathcal{R}_{1}q={\mathbb{P}}_{0}q.

(5.64)

Hence, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}_{0}[\mathcal{R}_{1},{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\bm{V}]\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\widetilde{\nabla}\nabla^{s}N=0.

(5.65)

The estimates (
3.9
), (
5.63
), and (
5.65
), along with Lemmas
3.2
and
4.1
, imply that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{122}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{3}\int_{0}^{t}\left(\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}[\mathcal{R}_{1},{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\bm{V}]\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}\nabla^{s}N\cdot N_{0,s}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|\right.

(5.66)

\displaystyle\left.+\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\left([\mathcal{R}_{1},{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\bm{V}]\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}\nabla^{s}N+[\mathcal{R}_{1},{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\bm{V}]\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\widetilde{\nabla}\nabla^{s}N\right)\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N_{0,s}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|\right)\,d\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{3}\Big(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\infty}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\nabla^{s}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\|N_{0,s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}(N,\bm{V})\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}(\nabla N,\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V})\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Recalling the definition (
3.12
), we apply Lemmas
3.1
,
3.3
,
4.1
, Sobolev inequality, and (
4.11
) to obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{123}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{3}\left[\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}\bm{V}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\nabla\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\right.

(5.67)

\displaystyle\left.\times\|N_{0,s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}(N,\bm{V})\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}(\widetilde{\nabla}N,\nabla\bm{V})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Substituting (
5.62
) (
5.66
) and (
5.67
) into (
5.60
), we find that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{12}\,d\tau\lesssim\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

(5.68)

With the help of Lemmas
3.1
,
4.1
, and Sobolev inequality, we readily check that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{13}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{3}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}(N,D)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}^{s}(N,D)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

(5.69)

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left[\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}D\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\|N_{0,s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}+\left(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}D\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}D\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}^{3}D\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\right)\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(\tau)\,d\tau.

Combining (
5.57
), (
5.59
), (
5.68
), and (
5.69
), one can deduce (
5.52
) directly.

5.2.2
Proof of Lemma
5.3

Recalling the definitions of
\mathcal{R}_{1}
and
\mathcal{R}_{5}
in (
3.12
) and (
3.14
), respectively, and referring to (
1.7
) and (
2.12
), we obtain

\displaystyle{\mathscr{F}}_{2,0,s}=\mathcal{R}_{1}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla^{s}{\mathscr{F}}_{2}=\mathcal{R}_{1}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla^{s}\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{F}_{2}=\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\nabla^{s}\bm{F}_{2}.

We begin by dealing with the second term by recalling (
1.7
) as follows

\displaystyle I_{2}\leq

\displaystyle\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\bm{F}_{2}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,0}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\nabla^{s}\bm{F}_{2}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

(5.70)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\left(\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}\right){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,0}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\nabla^{s}\left(\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}\right){\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\left(f(N)\widetilde{\nabla}N\right){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,0}+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\nabla^{s}\left(f(N)\widetilde{\nabla}N\right){\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\right)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\left(g(N)\left(\mu\widetilde{\Delta}\bm{V}+(\mu+\mu^{\prime})\widetilde{\nabla}\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V}\right)\right){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,0}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\nabla^{s}\left(g(N)\left(\mu\widetilde{\Delta}\bm{V}+(\mu+\mu^{\prime})\widetilde{\nabla}\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V}\right)\right){\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\right)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{4}I_{2i},

where
f(N)
and
g(N)
are defined in (
2.15
).
Recalling the definitions of
\mathcal{R}_{1}
,
\mathcal{R}_{5}
, and
{\mathcal{U}}_{0,0}
in (
3.12
), (
3.14
), and (
4.7
), respectively, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\left(\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}\right)=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\left[\bm{V}\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,0}+{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}[\mathcal{R}_{1},\bm{V}]\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}\mathcal{R}_{5}\bm{V}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\left([\mathcal{R}_{5},\bm{V}]\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}\right)+\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}V^{2}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}\bm{V})\right].

This estimate, together with integrating by parts, and Lemma
4.1
, leads us to get that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{21}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}}\left(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,0}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\right)\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,0}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

(5.71)

\displaystyle\quad+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}V^{2}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\infty}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}\bm{V}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,0}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

By integration by parts again, we find that

\displaystyle I_{22}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V}|{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}|^{2}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}[\mathcal{R}_{1},\bm{V}]\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}\nabla^{s}\mathcal{R}_{5}\bm{V}\cdot{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

(5.72)

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\mathcal{R}_{1}([\mathcal{R}_{5},\bm{V}]\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}\nabla^{s}\bm{V})\cdot{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}([\nabla^{s},\bm{V}]\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V})\cdot{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{4}I_{22i}.

Owing to
\mathcal{R}_{5}\bm{V}=\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}D
,
it then follows, in a similar manner to estimating (
5.62
) and (
5.66
), that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}(I_{221}+I_{222})(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

(5.73)

Notice that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\mathcal{R}_{5}\bm{V}={\mathbb{P}}_{0}\mathcal{R}_{4}V^{2},

(5.74)

which, together with (
5.64
), implies that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\mathcal{R}_{1}\left([\mathcal{R}_{5},{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\bm{V}]\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\widetilde{\nabla}\nabla^{s}\bm{V}\right)=[\mathcal{R}_{4},{\mathbb{P}}_{0}V^{2}]{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}^{s+1}V^{2}.

(5.75)

Using a similar decomposition as (
5.63
), and applying (
5.75
), we obtain that

\displaystyle I_{223}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}[\mathcal{R}_{4},{\mathbb{P}}_{0}V^{2}]\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}^{s+1}V^{2}\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{0}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

(5.76)

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\mathcal{R}_{1}[\mathcal{R}_{5},{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\bm{V}]\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}\nabla^{s}\bm{V}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left(\mathcal{R}_{1}[\mathcal{R}_{5},{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\bm{V}]\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}\nabla^{s}\bm{V}+\mathcal{R}_{1}[\mathcal{R}_{5},{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\bm{V}]\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\widetilde{\nabla}\nabla^{s}\bm{V}\right){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|.

By virtue of Lemma
3.1
, Lemma
3.2
, and Lemma
4.1
, we deduce from (
5.76
) that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{223}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{3}\left(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}V^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}^{s}V^{2}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\right.

(5.77)

\displaystyle+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\infty}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\nabla^{s}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\|{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\left.+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}\bm{V}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}(\nabla\bm{V},\widetilde{\nabla}V^{2})\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Again thanks to Lemma
3.1
, Lemma
3.3
, and Lemma
4.1
, along with (
5.74
), it then follows from a similar decomposition as (
5.63
) that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{224}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{3}\left(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}\bm{V}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}V^{2}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\nabla\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\right.

(5.78)

\displaystyle\left.\times\|{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}\bm{V}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}(\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V},\nabla\bm{V})\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Substituting (
5.73
) (
5.77
) and (
5.78
) into (
5.72
), we find that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{22}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

(5.79)

For
I_{23}
, we divide it as the following two terms:

\displaystyle I_{23}=

\displaystyle\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left[{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\left(\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\left(f(N)\widetilde{\nabla}N\right)\right){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,0}+\sum_{s=1}^{2}\left(\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\nabla^{s}\left(f(N)\widetilde{\nabla}N\right)\right){\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\right]\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

(5.80)

\displaystyle+\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left(\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\nabla^{3}\left(f(N)\widetilde{\nabla}N\right)\right){\mathcal{U}}_{0,3}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle I_{231}+I_{232}.

While it is easy to observe, by applying (
5.2
), Lemma
3.1
and Lemma
4.1
, that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{231}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{2}\left(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\right.

(5.81)

\displaystyle\left.\times\|{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}(\widetilde{\nabla}N,N)\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

As for
I_{232}
, notice that there exists a smooth function
\tilde{f}
, such that

\displaystyle\nabla^{3}(f(N)\widetilde{\nabla}N)=\nabla^{3}\widetilde{\nabla}(\tilde{f}(N)).

Thus, using Lemma
3.1
, Lemma
4.1
, and (
5.2
), we integrate by parts to deduce

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{232}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left(\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\left(\nabla^{3}(\tilde{f}(N))\right)\right)\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,3}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|\,d\tau

(5.82)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,3}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\|\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,3}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\left.+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,3}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

The combination of (
5.80
)-(
5.82
) yields that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{23}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim\mu^{-1}|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

(5.83)

Now we return to estimate
I_{24}
. By integration by parts, we obtain

\displaystyle I_{24}\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\left(g(N)\left(\mu\widetilde{\Delta}\bm{V}+(\mu+\mu^{\prime})\widetilde{\nabla}\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V}\right)\right){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,0}\right.\right.

(5.84)

\displaystyle\left.\left.+\sum_{s=1}^{2}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\nabla^{s}\left(g(N)\left(\mu\widetilde{\Delta}\bm{V}+(\mu+\mu^{\prime})\widetilde{\nabla}\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V}\right)\right){\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\right)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\nabla^{3}\left(g(N)\left(\mu\widetilde{\Delta}\bm{V}+(\mu+\mu^{\prime})\widetilde{\nabla}\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V}\right)\right){\mathcal{U}}_{0,3}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle I_{241}+I_{242}.

With the help of (
5.2
), Lemma
3.1
and Lemma
4.1
, we get

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{241}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu\sum_{s=1}^{2}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}^{2}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

(5.85)

\displaystyle+\mu\sum_{s=0}^{2}\left(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}^{2}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\left(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}^{2}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}^{2}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\right)\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-\frac{1}{3}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{1,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Noticing that
\nabla^{3}\left(\mu\widetilde{\Delta}\bm{V}+(\mu+\mu^{\prime})\widetilde{\nabla}\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V}\right)
in
I_{242}
cannot be controlled by all the energy in the
H^{3}
framework, we deal with this term by integration by parts and using the dissipation estimate of
D_{0,3}
, namely
\widetilde{\nabla}D_{0,3}
.
More precisely, we have

\displaystyle I_{242}\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\sum_{s=0}^{2}\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\nabla^{s}\left(\nabla g(N)\nabla^{2-s}\left(\mu\widetilde{\Delta}\bm{V}+(\mu+\mu^{\prime})\widetilde{\nabla}\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V}\right)\right){\mathcal{U}}_{0,3}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

(5.86)

\displaystyle+\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left[\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\left(g(N)\nabla^{3}\left(\mu\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}+(\mu+\mu^{\prime})\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V}\right)\right)\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,3}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.\quad+\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\left(\widetilde{\nabla}g(N)\nabla^{3}\left(\mu\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}+(\mu+\mu^{\prime})\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V}\right)\right){\mathcal{U}}_{0,3}\right]\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle I_{2421}+I_{2422}.

According to Lemma
3.1
, Lemma
4.1
, the Sobolev inequality, and (
5.2
), we see that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{2421}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}g(N)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}^{2}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,3}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}+\mu\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\nabla g(N)\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}

(5.87)

\displaystyle\times\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}^{2}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathcal{U}}_{0,3}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}+\mu\left(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}g(N)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}^{2}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\nabla g(N)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}^{2}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\right)\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,3}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-\frac{1}{3}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

For
I_{2422}
, invoking Lemma
3.1
, Lemma
4.1
, the Sobolev inequality, and (
5.2
), we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{2422}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}^{4}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\left(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,3}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,3}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\right.

(5.88)

\displaystyle\left.+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,3}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,3}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle+\mu\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}\nabla^{3}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\left(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,3}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathcal{U}}_{0,3}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,3}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,3}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{1,2}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Substituting (
5.87
) and (
5.88
) into (
5.86
), we deduce that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{242}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim\mu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{1,2}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

(5.89)

Substituting (
5.85
) and (
5.89
) into (
5.84
), we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{24}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim\mu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{1,2}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

(5.90)

The combination of (
5.71
), (
5.70
), (
5.79
), (
5.83
) and (
5.90
) yields (
5.53
) directly.

5.2.3
Proof of Lemma
5.4

Recalling (
2.11
), (
2.12
), (
3.14
), and (
5.74
) and integrating by parts, we decompose
I_{3}
into the following three terms:

\displaystyle I_{3}\leq

\displaystyle\mu\sum_{s=1}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{0}|\partial_{y_{1}}|^{-1}{\mathscr{F}}_{1,0,s}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}+{\mathbb{P}}_{0}{\mathscr{F}}_{2,0,s}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}|\partial_{y_{1}}|^{-1}N_{0,s}\right)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

(5.91)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu\sum_{s=0}^{2}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\mathcal{R}_{4}\partial_{y_{1}}^{s}\left(\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}N\right){\mathbb{P}}_{0}\mathcal{R}_{4}\partial_{y_{1}}^{s+1}V^{2}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle+\mu\sum_{s=0}^{2}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\mathcal{R}_{4}\partial_{y_{1}}^{s}\left(ND\right){\mathbb{P}}_{0}\mathcal{R}_{4}\partial_{y_{1}}^{s+1}V^{2}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle+\mu\sum_{s=0}^{2}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\mathcal{R}_{4}\partial_{y_{1}}^{s}\left(\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}V^{2}+f(N)\widetilde{\nabla}N\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+g(N)(\mu\widetilde{\Delta}\bm{V}+(\mu+\mu^{\prime})\widetilde{\nabla}\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V})\right){\mathbb{P}}_{0}\mathcal{R}_{4}\partial_{y_{1}}^{s+1}N\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}I_{3i}.

Then, we will handled all the terms
I_{3i}(i=1,\cdots,5)
.
Some direct computations lead us to get that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\left(\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}N\right)={\mathbb{P}}_{0}V^{2}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}N+{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\bm{V}\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}N\right).

We observe that
{\mathbb{P}}_{0}V^{1}
does not appear; therefore, according to the Sobolev inequality, Lemma
3.1
, and Lemma
4.1
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{31}(\tau)\,d\tau{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\mathcal{R}_{4}\partial_{y_{1}}^{s+1}V^{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu\sum_{s=0}^{2}\left(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}V^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}^{s+1}V^{2}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\right.

(5.92)

\displaystyle\left.+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}^{s+1}V^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Following a similar derivation as in (
5.92
), we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}(I_{32}+I_{33})(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

(5.93)

Collecting (
5.91
)-(
5.93
) together, we obtain (
5.54
) immediately.

5.2.4
Proof of Lemma
5.5

Unlike the estimate for
I_{3}
, the “bad derivative”
\widetilde{\nabla}
does not coincide with the “good derivative”
\nabla
on non-zero modes, so
\nabla
and
\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}
can not be canceled.
Moreover, neither
\nabla^{3}\widetilde{\nabla}N
nor
{\mathbb{P}}_{0}V^{1}
can be controlled by the energy we have defined.
Consequently, we must combine the two transport terms and integrate by parts to handle
I_{4}
. More precisely, using (
2.11
) and (
2.12
), we obtain

\displaystyle I_{4}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}{\mathscr{F}}_{1,0,s}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}+{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathscr{F}}_{2,0,s}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}_{3}N_{0,s}\right)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

(5.94)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{3}\nabla^{s}(\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}N){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}+{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\nabla^{s}(\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}N_{0,s}\right)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{3}\nabla^{s}(ND){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\nabla^{s}(f(N)\widetilde{\nabla}N){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}N_{0,s}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\nabla^{s}\left(g(N)(\mu\widetilde{\Delta}\bm{V}+(\mu+\mu^{\prime})\widetilde{\nabla}\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V})\right){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}N_{0,s}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{4}I_{4i}.

For the first term
I_{41}
, while it is easy to observe that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{3}\nabla^{s}(\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}N)=

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{3}\nabla^{s}({\mathbb{P}}_{0}\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N)+{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{3}\nabla^{s}({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}N)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}N_{0,s}+[\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{3},{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\bm{V}]\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\nabla^{s}N

\displaystyle+\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{3}\big([\nabla^{s},{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\bm{V}]\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\big)+{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{3}\nabla^{s}({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}N),

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\nabla^{s}(\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V})=

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\nabla^{s}({\mathbb{P}}_{0}\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\bm{V})+{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\nabla^{s}({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}+[\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5},{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\bm{V}]\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\nabla^{s}\bm{V}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\big([\nabla^{s},{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\bm{V}]\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\bm{V}\big)+{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\nabla^{s}({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}).

These estimates enable us to express
I_{41}
as the sum of four terms:

\displaystyle I_{41}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{0}\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}N_{0,s}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}+{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}N_{0,s}\right)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

(5.95)

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=0}^{3}\Big|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\big([\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{3},{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\bm{V}]\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\nabla^{s}N\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}

\displaystyle\qquad~~+[\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5},{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\bm{V}]\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\nabla^{s}\bm{V}\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}N_{0,s}\big)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\Big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\Big|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\big(\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{3}\big([\nabla^{s},{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\bm{V}]\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\big)\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}

\displaystyle\qquad~~+\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\big([\nabla^{s},{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\bm{V}]\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\bm{V}\big)\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}N_{0,s}\big)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\Big|

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=0}^{3}\Big|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\big({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{3}\nabla^{s}({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}N){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}

\displaystyle\qquad~~+{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\nabla^{s}({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}N_{0,s}\big)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\Big|

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{4}I_{41i}.

For
I_{411}
, by some direct calculations, we have

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}N_{0,s}=\widetilde{\Delta}^{\frac{1}{2}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{0}\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}N_{0,s}\right)-{\mathbb{P}}_{0}|\partial_{y_{1}}|\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}N_{0,s}.

(5.96)

Then, applying (
5.96
) and integrating by parts, we find that

\displaystyle I_{411}=

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{0}\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}N_{0,s}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\right.\right.

(5.97)

\displaystyle\left.\left.\qquad-{\mathbb{P}}_{0}|\partial_{y_{1}}|\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}N_{0,s}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}+{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}N_{0,s}\right)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\big({\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}V^{2}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}N_{0,s}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}+{\mathbb{P}}_{0}|\partial_{y_{1}}|\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}N_{0,s}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\big)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

Invoking Lemma
4.1
, we can deduce from (
5.97
) that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{411}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{3}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}\bm{V}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

(5.98)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(\tau)\,d\tau.

Thanks to Lemma
3.1
, Lemma
3.2
and Lemma
4.1
, we arrive at

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{412}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{3}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}\bm{V}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}}\left(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\nabla^{s}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\nabla^{s}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\right)

(5.99)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Invoking Lemmas
3.1
,
3.3
, and
4.1
, we conclude that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{413}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{3}\left(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}\bm{V}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\right.

(5.100)

\displaystyle\left.\qquad+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}\bm{V}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-\frac{5}{6}}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

As for
I_{414}
, while it is easy to observe that

\displaystyle{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{3}\nabla^{s}({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}N)={\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{3}\nabla^{s}\widetilde{\nabla}({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\bm{V}N)-{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{3}\nabla^{s}({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V}N).

(5.101)

Applying (
5.101
) and using integration by parts, we find that

\displaystyle I_{414}=

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{3}\nabla^{s}\widetilde{\nabla}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\bm{V}N){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\right.\right.

(5.102)

\displaystyle\qquad-{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{3}\nabla^{s}({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V}N){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}

\displaystyle\left.\left.\qquad+{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\nabla^{s}({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}N_{0,s}\right)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|.

Again thanks to Lemmas
3.1
and
4.1
, it follows from (
5.102
) that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{414}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\right)\|N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

(5.103)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{s=0}^{3}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\bm{V}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}\|\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Substituting (
5.98
), (
5.99
), (
5.100
) and (
5.103
) into (
5.95
), we find that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{41}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

(5.104)

It then follows from Lemma
3.1
and Lemma
4.1
that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{42}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}D\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}\|D\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\right)\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(\tau)\,d\tau.

(5.105)

Integrating by parts, together with (
5.2
), Lemma
3.1
, and Lemma
4.1
, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{43}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{3}\int_{0}^{t}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\widetilde{\nabla}(\tilde{f}(N)){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}N_{0,s}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|\,d\tau

(5.106)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{3}\int_{0}^{t}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\tilde{f}(N){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}N_{0,s}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|\,d\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{3}\|N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-\frac{2}{3}}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{1,2}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

For
I_{44}
, one can readily see that

\displaystyle\nabla^{s}\left(g(N)(\mu\widetilde{\Delta}\bm{V}+(\mu+\mu^{\prime})\widetilde{\nabla}\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V})\right)

(5.107)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle g(N)\nabla^{s}\left(\mu\widetilde{\Delta}\bm{V}+(\mu+\mu^{\prime})\widetilde{\nabla}\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V}\right)+[\nabla^{s},g(N)]\left(\mu\widetilde{\Delta}\bm{V}+(\mu+\mu^{\prime})\widetilde{\nabla}\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V}\right).

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\left(g(N)\nabla^{s}\left(\mu\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}+(\mu+\mu^{\prime})\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V}{\mathbb{I}}\right)\right)+\widetilde{\nabla}g(N)\nabla^{s}\left(\mu\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}+(\mu+\mu^{\prime})\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V}{\mathbb{I}}\right)

\displaystyle+[\nabla^{s},g(N)]\left(\mu\widetilde{\Delta}\bm{V}+(\mu+\mu^{\prime})\widetilde{\nabla}\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V}\right).

Applying (
5.107
) and integration by parts again, it is easy to check that

\displaystyle I_{44}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\left(g(N)\nabla^{s}\left(\mu\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}+(\mu+\mu^{\prime})\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V}{\mathbb{I}}\right)\right){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}N_{0,s}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\left(\widetilde{\nabla}g(N)\nabla^{s}\left(\mu\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}+(\mu+\mu^{\prime})\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V}{\mathbb{I}}\right)\right){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}N_{0,s}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{1}\mathcal{R}_{5}\left([\nabla^{s},g(N)]\left(\mu\widetilde{\Delta}\bm{V}+(\mu+\mu^{\prime})\widetilde{\nabla}\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\bm{V}\right)\right){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}N_{0,s}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|.

This, together with (
5.2
), Lemmas
3.1
,
3.3
and
4.1
, implies that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}I_{44}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu\sum_{s=0}^{3}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\nabla^{s}\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

(5.108)

\displaystyle+\mu\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\right)\|\nabla^{s}\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\mu\sum_{s=1}^{3}\left(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\nabla N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}^{2}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\nabla N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|\widetilde{\nabla}^{2}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\right)\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-\frac{1}{3}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Substituting (
5.104
), (
5.105
), (
5.106
) and (
5.108
) into (
5.94
), we obtain (
5.55
).

5.2.5
Proof of Lemma
5.6

It is easy to check that

\displaystyle|I_{5}|\leq\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathscr{F}}_{1,0,s}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}{\mathcal{U}}_{0,s}+{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathscr{F}}_{2,0,s}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}N_{0,s}\right)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|.

Throughout the proof of
I_{4}
, we treat
\mathcal{R}_{3}
as a bounded operator. Proceeding exactly as in the proof of (
5.55
), we immediately obtain (
5.56
); the details are omitted for brevity.

6
Energy estimate of incompressible part

We observe that
{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)
—the incompressible part of the solution—appears on the right–hand side of (
5.3
) and remains to be controlled. Accordingly, the present subsection is devoted to deriving the corresponding
a priori
energy estimates for the higher-order derivatives of the vorticity, as stated in Proposition
6.1
.

Proposition 6.1
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
2.2
and (
5.1
), we have

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)+\int_{0}^{t}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle~{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(0)+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}\int_{0}^{t}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}(\tau)\,d\tau+\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{1,2}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right.

(6.1)

\displaystyle\left.+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{5}{6}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

6.1
Proof of Proposition
6.1

Before proceeding, we state the two key propositions on which the proof of Proposition
6.1
relies.

Since no
L^{2}
estimate is available for
{\mathbb{P}}_{0}V^{1}
, we cannot bound
\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\|_{L^{2}}
in
L^{2}_{t}
; only an
L^{\infty}_{t}
bound is attainable. This renders the transport term
{\mathbb{P}}_{0}V^{2}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}W
difficult to control directly.
Inspired by the treatment of
({\mathbb{P}}_{0}N,{\mathbb{P}}_{0}V^{2})
in the previous section, we derive a separate
L^{2}
estimate for
{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W
and exploit the density equation to cancel problematic terms such as
{\mathbb{P}}_{0}V^{2}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}W
. Accordingly, we introduce the following definitions.

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{3}(t)\triangleq\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}|\hat{W}_{0,0}^{0}|^{2}\,d\xi,

(6.2)

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{4}(t)\triangleq\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}|\hat{W}_{0,0}^{k}|^{2}\,d\xi+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}|\hat{W}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}\,d\xi,,

(6.3)

and

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{3}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle\mu\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}|\xi|^{2}|\hat{W}_{0,0}^{0}|^{2}\,d\xi,

(6.4)

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{4}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\mu p\right)|\hat{W}_{0,0}^{k}|^{2}\,d\xi

(6.5)

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\mu p\right)|\hat{W}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}\,d\xi,

where
\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{3}(t)
and
\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{3}(t)
are the
L^{2}
estimate for the zero-mode of the vorticity. And
\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{4}(t)
and
\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{4}(t)
involve
the remaining terms in
{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)
and
{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)
.
The straightforward calculations yields that

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)\sim\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{3}(t)+\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{4}(t),\quad{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)\sim\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{3}(t)+\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{4}(t).

(6.6)

From these results, Proposition
6.1
follows once we combine Propositions
6.2
and Propositions
6.3
.

We begin by deriving the
L^{2}
estimate for the zero-mode of the vorticity.

Proposition 6.2
.

Under the assumptions of Proposition
6.1
, we have

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{3}(t)+\int_{0}^{t}\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{3}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle~\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{3}(0)+\mu^{2}\int_{0}^{t}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}(\tau)\,d\tau+\mu^{-1}\left(1+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

(6.7)

\displaystyle\times\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Next, we estimate the remaining terms in
{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)
and
{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)
, namely
\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{4}(t)
and
\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{4}(t)
.

Proposition 6.3
.

Under the assumptions of Proposition
6.1
, we have

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{4}(t)+\int_{0}^{t}\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{4}(t)(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle~\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{4}(0)+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}\int_{0}^{t}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}(\tau)\,d\tau

(6.8)

\displaystyle+\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{1,2}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{5}{6}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

6.2
Proof of Proposition
6.2

We apply the operator
{\mathbb{P}}_{0}
to
\eqref{eq_n-d-w-1}_{3}
, to obtain that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W-\mu\partial_{y_{1}}^{2}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W+\mu(\mu+\mu^{\prime})\partial_{y_{1}}^{2}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}D=-\partial_{y_{1}}({\mathbb{P}}_{0}W{\mathbb{P}}_{0}V^{2})+G_{3},

(6.9)

where
G_{3}
is defined by

\displaystyle G_{3}\triangleq

\displaystyle-{\mathbb{P}}_{0}({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\bm{V}\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}W+{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}D{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}W)-\mu{\mathbb{P}}_{0}D^{2}

(6.10)

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}\left(g(N)(\nu\partial_{x_{1}}D-\mu(\partial_{y_{1}}-t\partial_{x_{1}})(W-N+\mu D))\right)+\mu G_{31},

\displaystyle G_{31}\triangleq

\displaystyle(\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}V^{2})^{2}+{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\left[({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\partial_{x_{1}}V^{1})^{2}+({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}(\partial_{y_{1}}-t\partial_{x_{1}})V^{2})^{2}+2{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}(\partial_{y_{1}}-t\partial_{x_{1}})V^{1}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\partial_{x_{1}}V^{2}\right]

(6.11)

\displaystyle+{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}\left(f(N)(\partial_{y_{1}}-t\partial_{x_{1}})N\right)+{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}\left(g(N)(\nu(\partial_{y_{1}}-t\partial_{x_{1}})D+\mu\partial_{x_{1}}(W-N+\mu D))\right).

Then applying
{\mathbb{P}}_{0}
to
\eqref{eq_n-d-w-1}_{1}
, we get

\displaystyle\partial_{t}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N+{\mathbb{P}}_{0}D={\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}(NV^{2}).

(6.12)

Proof of Proposition
6.2
.

Applying the standard
L^{2}
energy estimate to equation (
6.9
), we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\mu\|\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

(6.13)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\mu(\mu+\mu^{\prime})\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\partial_{y_{1}}^{2}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}D{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\,dy_{1}-\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\partial_{y_{1}}({\mathbb{P}}_{0}W{\mathbb{P}}_{0}V^{2}){\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\,dy_{1}+\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}G_{3}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\,dy_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu(\mu+\mu^{\prime})\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}D\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\,dy_{1}-\frac{1}{2}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}V^{2}|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W|^{2}\,dy_{1}+\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}G_{3}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\,dy_{1}.

In order to eliminate the bad term
\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}V^{2}|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W|^{2}\,dy_{1}
on the right-hand side of (
6.13
), we multiply (
6.12
) by
\frac{1}{2}|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W|^{2}
, to deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}V^{2}|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W|^{2}\,dy_{1}

(6.14)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\partial_{t}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W|^{2}\,dy_{1}-\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}(NV^{2})|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W|^{2}\,dy_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W|^{2}\,dy_{1}+\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\partial_{t}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\,dy_{1}-\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}(NV^{2})|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W|^{2}\,dy_{1}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W|^{2}\,dy_{1}+\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\left(\mu{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}^{2}W-\mu(\mu+\mu^{\prime}){\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}^{2}D\right)\,dy_{1}

\displaystyle-\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\partial_{y_{1}}({\mathbb{P}}_{0}W{\mathbb{P}}_{0}V^{2})\,dy_{1}+\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N{\mathbb{P}}_{0}WG_{3}\,dy_{1}-\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}(NV^{2})|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W|^{2}\,dy_{1}.

Collecting (
6.13
) and (
6.14
) together, we achieve that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}(1-{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N)|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W|^{2}\,dy_{1}+\mu\|\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

(6.15)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu(\mu+\mu^{\prime})\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}D\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\,dy_{1}-\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\left(\mu{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}^{2}W-\mu(\mu+\mu^{\prime}){\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}^{2}D\right)\,dy_{1}

\displaystyle+\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\partial_{y_{1}}({\mathbb{P}}_{0}W{\mathbb{P}}_{0}V^{2})\,dy_{1}+\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}G_{3}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W(1-{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N)\,dy_{1}+\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}(NV^{2})|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W|^{2}\,dy_{1}

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{5}{\mathcal{J}}_{i}.

By Young’s inequality, we readily obtain

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{J}}_{1}|\leq

\displaystyle\widetilde{\epsilon}\mu\|\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\widetilde{\epsilon}}\mu^{3}\|\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}D\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\leq\widetilde{\epsilon}\mu\|\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\widetilde{\epsilon}}\mu^{2}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}.

(6.16)

Integration by parts yields that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|{\mathcal{J}}_{2}|(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left|\partial_{y_{1}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{0}N{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\right)\left(\mu{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}W-\mu(\mu+\mu^{\prime}){\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}D\right)\right|\,dy_{1}d\tau

(6.17)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}(N,W)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}}\|\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}(N,W)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\left(\|\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}+\mu\|\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}D\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

In light of Sobolev inequality and Lemma
4.1
, we get

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|{\mathcal{J}}_{3}|(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}(N,W)\|_{L^{4}_{t}L^{\infty}}^{2}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}(W,V^{2})\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}\|\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}(W,V^{2})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

(6.18)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}(N,W)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}\|\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}(N,W)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}(W,V^{2})\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}\|\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}(W,V^{2})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}\left({\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)+{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Recalling the definition of
G_{3}
in (
6.10
), we can infer that

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{J}}_{4}|=

\displaystyle\left|\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}(1-{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N){\mathbb{P}}_{0}W{\mathbb{P}}_{0}({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\bm{V}\cdot{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}W+{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}D{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}W)\,dy_{1}\right|+\mu\left|\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}(1-{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N){\mathbb{P}}_{0}W{\mathbb{P}}_{0}D^{2}\,dy_{1}\right|

(6.19)

\displaystyle+\left|\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}(1-{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N){\mathbb{P}}_{0}W{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}\left(g(N)(\nu\partial_{x_{1}}D-\mu(\partial_{y_{1}}-t\partial_{x_{1}})(W-N+\mu D))\right)\,dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle+\mu\left|\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}(1-{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N){\mathbb{P}}_{0}WG_{31}\,dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{4}{\mathcal{J}}_{4i}.

We then estimate
{\mathcal{J}}_{4i}

(i=1,\cdots,4)
term by term.
Again thanks to Lemma
4.1
and Sobolev inequality, we find that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|{\mathcal{J}}_{41}|(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle(1+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}})\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\big(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}W\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

(6.20)

\displaystyle+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}D\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}W\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-\frac{5}{6}}\left(1+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau,

and

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|{\mathcal{J}}_{42}|(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu(1+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}})\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}}(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}D\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}^{2}+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}D\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}^{2})

(6.21)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\left(1+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(\tau)\,d\tau.

By integrating by parts, one obtains that

\displaystyle{\mathcal{J}}_{43}\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu\left|\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\partial_{y_{1}}[(1-{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N){\mathbb{P}}_{0}W]\left({\mathbb{P}}_{0}g(N)\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}(W-N+\mu D)\right)\,dy_{1}\right|

(6.22)

\displaystyle+\left|\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}(1-{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N){\mathbb{P}}_{0}W{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}g(N){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}(\nu\partial_{x_{1}}D-\mu(\partial_{y_{1}}-t\partial_{x_{1}})(W-N+\mu D))\right)\,dy_{1}\right|

Invoking Lemma
4.1
and Sobolev inequality, we infer from (
6.22
) that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|{\mathcal{J}}_{43}|(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu(1+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}(N,W)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}})\|\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}(N,W)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

(6.23)

\displaystyle\times\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}(W-N+\mu D)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}+\mu(1+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}})\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}

\displaystyle\times\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{1}}\big(\|\nabla{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}D\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{1}}+\|\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}(W-N+\mu D)\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{1}}\big)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Remembering the definition of
G_{31}
in (
6.11
), we can get that

\displaystyle|{\mathcal{J}}_{44}|\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu\left|\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}(1-{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N){\mathbb{P}}_{0}W(\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}V^{2})^{2}\,dy_{1}\right|

(6.24)

\displaystyle+\mu\Big|\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}(1-{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N){\mathbb{P}}_{0}W{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\left[({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\partial_{x_{1}}V^{1})^{2}+({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}(\partial_{y_{1}}-t\partial_{x_{1}})V^{2})^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad+2{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}(\partial_{y_{1}}-t\partial_{x_{1}})V^{1}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\partial_{x_{1}}V^{2}\right]\,dy_{1}\Big|

\displaystyle+\mu\left|\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}(1-{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N){\mathbb{P}}_{0}W{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}\left(f(N)(\partial_{y_{1}}-t\partial_{x_{1}})N\right)\,dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle+\mu\left|\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}(1-{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N){\mathbb{P}}_{0}W{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}\left(g(N)(\nu(\partial_{y_{1}}-t\partial_{x_{1}})D+\mu\partial_{x_{1}}(W-N+\mu D))\right)\,dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{4}{\mathcal{J}}_{44i}.

With the help of Soboev’s inequality and Lemma
4.1
, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|{\mathcal{J}}_{441}|(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu(1+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}})\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}D\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}^{2}

(6.25)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(\tau)\,d\tau,

and

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|{\mathcal{J}}_{442}|(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu(1+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}})\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}^{2}

(6.26)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\left(1+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

The derivation proceeds similarly to (
6.22
) and (
6.23
), yielding

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|{\mathcal{J}}_{443}|(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu\big(1+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}(N,W)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\big)\|\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}(N,W)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

(6.27)

\displaystyle+\mu\big(1+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\big)\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{1}}\|\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(1+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Employing the same manner as in (
6.22
) and (
6.23
) again, we conclude that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|{\mathcal{J}}_{444}|(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{2}(1+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}(N,W)\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}})\|\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}(N,W)\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}D\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

(6.28)

\displaystyle+\mu^{2}(1+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}})\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{1}}

\displaystyle\times\left(\|\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}D\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{1}}+\|\nabla{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}(W-N+\mu D)\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{1}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}\left(1+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Substituting (
6.25
)-(
6.28
) into (
6.24
), we achieve that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|{\mathcal{J}}_{44}|(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\left(1+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}

(6.29)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Plugging (
6.20
)-(
6.23
) and (
6.29
) into (
6.19
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|{\mathcal{J}}_{4}|(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-\frac{5}{6}}\left(1+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

(6.30)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Following the same approach as for
{\mathcal{J}}_{3}
, we bound
{\mathcal{J}}_{5}
as follows:

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}|{\mathcal{J}}_{5}|(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim
