\displaystyle\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}(N,V^{2})\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}\|\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}(N,V^{2})\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}\|\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

(6.31)

\displaystyle+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}(N,V^{2})\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}(N,V^{2})\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{1}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}\left({\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)+{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Finally, we can conclude that (
6.7
) holds by collecting (
6.16
)-(
6.18
), (
6.30
), and (
6.31
) together and choosing
\widetilde{\epsilon}
suitably small.
∎

6.3
Proof of Proposition
6.3

Recalling the definition (
4.8
) for
j=0
, we then deduce from the equation
\eqref{eq_n-d-w-2}_{3}
that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\hat{W}_{0,s}^{k}=

\displaystyle-\left[\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\mu p\right]\hat{W}_{0,s}^{k}-2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}+\mu(\mu+\mu^{\prime})p\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}

(6.32)

\displaystyle-\mu\left(\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}-2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}\right)\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}+2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}\hat{W}_{0,s}^{k}+\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{3,1,s}^{k},

where

\displaystyle\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{3,1,s}^{k}\triangleq

\displaystyle m_{1}^{-1}m_{2}^{-1}<k,\xi>^{s}\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{3}^{k}.

(6.33)

Proof of Proposition
6.3
.

It follows from (
6.32
) that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}|\hat{W}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}+\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\mu p\right)|\hat{W}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}

(6.34)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\mu(\mu+\mu^{\prime})pRe\left(\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)-2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)

\displaystyle-\mu\left(\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}-2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}\right)Re\left(\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)+2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}|\hat{W}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{3,1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{0,s}^{k}\right).

Applying (
5.42
) and Young’s inequality again, the linear terms on the right-hand side of (
6.34
) can be controlled by

\displaystyle\left|\mu(\mu+\mu^{\prime})pRe\left(\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right|\lesssim\widetilde{\epsilon}\mu p|\hat{W}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}+C_{\widetilde{\epsilon}}\mu^{3}p|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2},

(6.35)

\displaystyle\left|2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right|\lesssim\widetilde{\epsilon}\frac{k^{2}}{p}|\hat{W}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}+C_{\widetilde{\epsilon}}\mu^{2}\frac{k^{2}}{p}|\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}|^{2},

(6.36)

\displaystyle\left|\mu\left(\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}-2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}\right)Re\left(\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right|\lesssim\widetilde{\epsilon}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}|\hat{W}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}+C_{\widetilde{\epsilon}}\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}\frac{k^{2}}{p}|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}.

(6.37)

Employing (
3.1
), and plugging all the estimates above into (
6.34
), then choosing
\widetilde{\epsilon}
suitably small and
A
defined in (
3.1
) suitably large, finally performing the summation and integration, we arrive at

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{4}(t)+\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{4}(t)\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left\{\mu^{2}\frac{k^{2}}{p}|\hat{N}_{1,0}^{k}|^{2}+\mu^{3}p|\hat{D}_{0,0}^{k}|^{2}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}\frac{k^{2}}{p}|\hat{D}_{0,0}^{k}|^{2}+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{3,1,0}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{0,0}^{k}\right)\right\}\,d\xi

(6.38)

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left\{\mu^{2}\frac{k^{2}}{p}|\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}|^{2}+\mu^{3}p|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}\frac{k^{2}}{p}|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{3,1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right\}\,d\xi

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}(t)+\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathscr{F}}_{3,1,0}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}W_{0,0}+\sum_{s=1}^{3}{\mathscr{F}}_{3,1,s}W_{0,s}\right)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|,

where
\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{4}(t)
and
\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{4}(t)
are defined by (
6.3
) and (
6.5
), respectively.

We now begin to estimate the nonlinear term on the right-hand side of (
6.38
).

\displaystyle\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathscr{F}}_{3,1,0}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}W_{0,0}+\sum_{s=1}^{3}{\mathscr{F}}_{3,1,s}W_{0,s}\right)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

(6.39)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{2}(\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}W){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}W_{0,0}+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\mathcal{R}_{2}\nabla^{s}(\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}W)W_{0,s}\right)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{2}(DW){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}W_{0,0}+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\mathcal{R}_{2}\nabla^{s}(DW)W_{0,s}\right)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle+\mu\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{2}(D^{2}){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}W_{0,0}+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\mathcal{R}_{2}\nabla^{s}(D^{2})W_{0,s}\right)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle+\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{2}\left(\widetilde{\nabla}^{\perp}\cdot\left(g(N)(\nu\widetilde{\nabla}D+\mu\widetilde{\nabla}^{\perp}(W-N+\nu D))\right)\right){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}W_{0,0}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\mathcal{R}_{2}\nabla^{s}\left(\widetilde{\nabla}^{\perp}\cdot\left(g(N)(\nu\widetilde{\nabla}D+\mu\widetilde{\nabla}^{\perp}(W-N+\nu D))\right)\right)W_{0,s}\right)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle+\mu\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{2}\nabla^{s}\widetilde{F}_{21}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}W_{0,s}+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\mathcal{R}_{2}\nabla^{s}\widetilde{F}_{21}W_{0,s}\right)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{5}J_{i},

where
\widetilde{F}_{21}
is defined in (
2.14
).
By a similar derivation as in (
5.59
) and (
5.68
), we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}J_{1}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{1,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}+{\mathscr{D}}_{1,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

(6.40)

Using simialr decomposition as (
5.63
), together with Lemma
3.1
and Sobolev inequality, we deduce that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}J_{2}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{3}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}D\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

(6.41)

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=0}^{3}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}D\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}W\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\|W_{0,s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}D\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}W\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}D\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}\right)\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}W_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Again thanks to Sobolev inequality and Lemma
3.1
, by a similar procedure, we find that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}J_{3}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu\sum_{s=1}^{3}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}D\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\infty}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\nabla^{s}D\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W_{0,s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}

(6.42)

\displaystyle+\mu\sum_{s=0}^{3}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}D\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\infty}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\nabla^{s}D\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\|W_{0,s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\mu\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left[\left(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}D\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\nabla^{s}D\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}D\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\nabla^{s}D\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\right)\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}W_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{1,2}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Invoking the Sobolev inequality and Lemma
3.1
once more, we integrate by parts to obtain

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}J_{4}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu\sum_{s=1}^{3}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}(D,W,N)\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}W_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

(6.43)

\displaystyle+\mu\sum_{s=0}^{3}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}(D,W,N)\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\|\widetilde{\nabla}W_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\mu\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}(D,W,N)\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}(D,W,N)\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\right)\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}W_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{5}{6}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

In order to bound the last term

, we split it into the following three parts:

\displaystyle J_{5}\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\Big({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{2}\left[(\partial_{x_{1}}V^{1})^{2}+((\partial_{y_{1}}-t\partial_{x_{1}})V^{2})^{2}+2(\partial_{y_{1}}-t\partial_{x_{1}})V^{1}\partial_{x_{1}}V^{2}\right]{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}W_{0,0}\right.

(6.44)

\displaystyle\left.+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\mathcal{R}_{2}\nabla^{s}\left[(\partial_{x_{1}}V^{1})^{2}+((\partial_{y_{1}}-t\partial_{x_{1}})V^{2})^{2}+2(\partial_{y_{1}}-t\partial_{x_{1}})V^{1}\partial_{x_{1}}V^{2}\right]W_{0,s}\Big)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle+\mu\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{2}\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\left(f(N)\widetilde{\nabla}N\right){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}W_{0,0}+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\mathcal{R}_{2}\nabla^{s}\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\left(f(N)\widetilde{\nabla}N\right)W_{0,s}\right)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle+\mu\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{2}\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\left(g(N)(\nu\widetilde{\nabla}D+\mu\widetilde{\nabla}^{\perp}(W-N+\nu D))\right){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}W_{0,0}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\mathcal{R}_{2}\nabla^{s}\widetilde{\mathop{\rm div}\nolimits}\left(g(N)(\nu\widetilde{\nabla}D+\mu\widetilde{\nabla}^{\perp}(W-N+\nu D))\right)W_{0,s}\right)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

\displaystyle\triangleq

\displaystyle J_{51}+J_{52}+J_{53}.

Then we estimate
J_{5i}(i=1,2,3)
term by term.
Since
{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{x_{1}}q=0
, we observe that

\displaystyle J_{51}\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu\sum_{s=1}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\mathcal{R}_{2}\partial_{y_{1}}^{s}(\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}V^{2})^{2}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W_{0,s}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|

(6.45)

\displaystyle+\mu\sum_{s=0}^{3}\Big|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\mathcal{R}_{2}\nabla^{s}[(\partial_{x_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}V^{1})^{2}+((\partial_{y_{1}}-t\partial_{x_{1}}){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}V^{2})^{2}

\displaystyle\qquad+2(\partial_{y_{1}}-t\partial_{x_{1}}){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}V^{1}\partial_{x_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}V^{2}]W_{0,s}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\Big|

\displaystyle+\mu\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\mathcal{R}_{2}\nabla^{s}\left[\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}V^{2}(\partial_{y_{1}}-t\partial_{x_{1}}){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}V^{2}+2\partial_{y_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{0}V^{1}\partial_{x_{1}}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}V^{2}\right]{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}W_{0,s}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|.

Then employing Lemma
3.1
and Sobolev inequality, we can deduce from (
6.45
) that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}J_{51}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu\sum_{s=1}^{3}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}V^{2}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}^{s+1}V^{2}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}W_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

(6.46)

\displaystyle+\mu\sum_{s=0}^{3}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\infty}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\nabla^{s}\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\|W_{0,s}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\mu\sum_{s=0}^{3}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}\bm{V}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\nabla^{s}\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}W_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\mu\sum_{s=0}^{2}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{\infty}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}^{s+1}\bm{V}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}W_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\mu\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}\bm{V}\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}L^{\infty}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}^{4}\bm{V}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}W_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Regarding the term
J_{52}
, integrating by parts, we have

\displaystyle J_{52}=\mu\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{2}\left(f(N)\widetilde{\nabla}N\right)\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}W_{0,0}+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\mathcal{R}_{2}\nabla^{s}\left(f(N)\widetilde{\nabla}N\right)\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}W_{0,s}\right)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|.

(6.47)

Invoking Lemma
3.1
together with the Sobolev inequality, we deduce from (

) that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}J_{52}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu\sum_{s=1}^{3}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\widetilde{\nabla}W_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

(6.48)

\displaystyle+\mu\sum_{s=0}^{3}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\|\widetilde{\nabla}W_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\mu\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}\right)\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}W_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-\frac{5}{6}}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{5}{6}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Following a similar way as
J_{52}
, one gets that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}J_{53}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{2}\sum_{s=1}^{3}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}(D+W+N)\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}W_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

(6.49)

\displaystyle+\mu^{2}\sum_{s=0}^{3}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}(D+W+N)\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\|\widetilde{\nabla}W_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

\displaystyle+\mu^{2}\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left(\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}(D+W+N)\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}N\|_{L^{\infty}_{t}H^{3}}\|{\mathbb{P}}_{0}\partial_{y_{1}}(D+W+N)\|_{L^{2}_{t}H^{3}}\right)\|{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\nabla}W_{0,s}\|_{L^{2}_{t}L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{1,2}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Substituting (
6.46
), (
6.48
) and (
6.49
) into (
6.44
), we find that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}J_{5}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-\frac{5}{6}}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{1,2}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{5}{6}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

(6.50)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Plugging (
6.40
), (
6.41
), (
6.42
), (
6.43
) and (
6.50
) into (
6.39
), we arrive at

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}{\mathscr{F}}_{3,1,0}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}W_{0,0}+\sum_{s=1}^{3}{\mathscr{F}}_{3,1,s}W_{0,s}\right)\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|\,d\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{1,2}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{5}{6}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

This, along with (
6.38
), yields (
6.8
) immediately.
∎

7
Higher order energy estimate of compressible part

We observe that the term
{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}(t)
, appearing on the right-hand side of (
6.1
), has yet to be estimated. To close the energy estimates, we therefore need to bound the high-order derivatives of the density and the divergence, namely
{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com}(t)
and
{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}(t)
.
We formulate the required bounds in the following proposition.

Proposition 7.1
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
2.2
and (
5.1
), we have

\displaystyle\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com}(t)+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}\int_{0}^{t}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}(\tau)\,d\tau

(7.1)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com}(0)+\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\int_{0}^{t}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(\tau)\,d\tau+\frac{1}{A}\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}\int_{0}^{t}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}(\tau)\,d\tau

\displaystyle+\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{1,2}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{5}{6}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\times\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Before proving Proposition
7.1
, we first recall definitions (
4.9
) and (
4.10
) with
j=0
, and then derive from equations
\eqref{eq_n-d-w-2}_{1}
and
\eqref{eq_n-d-w-2}_{2}
that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}=

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}\right)\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}-p^{\frac{1}{2}}\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}+\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,1,s}^{k},

(7.2)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}=

\displaystyle-\left[\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\nu p\right]\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}+\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}+\left(p^{\frac{1}{2}}+2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right)\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}

(7.3)

\displaystyle\quad-2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}-2\frac{k^{2}}{p}\hat{W}_{0,s}^{k}+\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,1,s}^{k},

where
\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,1,s}^{k}
and
\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,1,s}^{k}
are defined by

\displaystyle\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,1,s}^{k}\triangleq

\displaystyle m_{1}^{-1}m_{2}^{-1}<k,\xi>^{s}p^{\frac{1}{2}}\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1}^{k},

(7.4)

\displaystyle\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,1,s}^{k}\triangleq

\displaystyle m_{1}^{-1}m_{2}^{-1}<k,\xi>^{s}\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2}^{k}.

(7.5)

By some direct computations, we can deduce from (
7.2
) and (
7.3
) that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}|(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k},\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k})|^{2}+\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}\right)|\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}|^{2}+\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\nu p\right)|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}

(7.6)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}|\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}|^{2}+\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}+2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)-2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}-2\frac{k^{2}}{p}Re\left(\hat{W}_{0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)

\displaystyle+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{N}}_{1,s}^{k}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right).

To cancel the first term on the right-hand side of (
7.6
), we require

\displaystyle-\frac{d}{dt}\left[\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right]

(7.7)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}|\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}|^{2}+\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}-2\frac{k^{2}\partial_{t}p}{p^{3}}|\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}|^{2}

\displaystyle+\left[\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\left(2\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+2\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\nu p\right)+2\mu\frac{k^{2}\partial_{t}p}{p^{\frac{5}{2}}}-2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right]Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)

\displaystyle+2\frac{k^{2}\partial_{t}p}{p^{\frac{5}{2}}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)-\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\left[Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{N}}_{1,s}^{k}\right)\right].

Multiplying (
7.7
) by
1/2
, adding the resulting equality to (
7.6
), finally performing the summation and integration, we find that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{s=0}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left[|\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}-\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right]\,d\xi

(7.8)

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=0}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}\right)|\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}|^{2}\,d\xi+\sum_{s=0}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\nu p\right)|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}\,d\xi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\Big\{-\frac{k^{2}\partial_{t}p}{p^{3}}|\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}|^{2}+\left(\frac{3}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}-2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}\right)|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\Big[\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\nu p\right)+\mu\frac{k^{2}\partial_{t}p}{p^{\frac{5}{2}}}+\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\Big]Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)-2\frac{k^{2}}{p}Re\left(\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\Big\}\,d\xi

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{s=0}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left[Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{N}}_{1,s}^{k}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right]\,d\xi

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{s=0}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\left[Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{N}}_{1,s}^{k}\right)\right]\,d\xi.

For the sake of obtaining the dissipation for
\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}
, we infer that

\displaystyle-\frac{d}{dt}\left[p^{-\frac{1}{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right]+\left(1+2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{2}}\right)|\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}|^{2}

(7.9)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}+\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(2\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+2\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}-\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}+2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}+\nu p\right)Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)

\displaystyle+2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)-\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left[Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{N}}_{1,s}^{k}\right)\right].

For
k\not=0
, we multiply (
7.9
) by
\delta_{2}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}
, then integrate over
\xi
in
{\mathbb{R}}
and sum over
k
in
{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}
, to find that

\displaystyle-\delta_{2}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{s=0}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left[p^{-\frac{1}{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right]\,d\xi+\delta_{2}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\sum_{s=0}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left(1+2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{2}}\right)|\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}|^{2}\,d\xi

(7.10)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{2}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\sum_{s=0}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left\{|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}+\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(2\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+2\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}-\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}+2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}+\nu p\right)Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.+2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)-\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left[Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{N}}_{1,s}^{k}\right)\right]\right\}\,d\xi.

While for
k=0
, we multiply (
7.9
) by
\delta_{2}\mu
, and integrate over
\xi
in
{\mathbb{R}}
, to get that

\displaystyle-\delta_{2}\mu\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{s=1}^{3}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left[p^{-\frac{1}{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{0}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{0}\right)\right]\,d\xi+\delta_{2}\mu\sum_{s=1}^{3}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}|\hat{N}_{1,s}^{0}|^{2}

(7.11)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{2}\mu\sum_{s=1}^{3}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left\{|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{0}|^{2}+\nu|\xi|Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{0}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{0}\right)-\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left[Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,1,s}^{0}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{0}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,1,s}^{0}\bar{\hat{N}}_{1,s}^{0}\right)\right]\right\}\,d\xi.

We define

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{5}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left[|\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}-\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right]\,d\xi

\displaystyle-2\delta_{2}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\sum_{s=0}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}p^{-\frac{1}{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\,d\xi-2\delta_{2}\mu\sum_{s=1}^{3}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}p^{-\frac{1}{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{0}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{0}\right)\,d\xi,

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{5}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\delta_{2}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\left(1+2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{2}}\right)\right)|\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}|^{2}\,d\xi+\delta_{2}\mu\sum_{s=1}^{3}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}|\hat{N}_{1,s}^{0}|^{2}\,d\xi

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=0}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\nu p\right)|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}\,d\xi.

By a few calculations, we obtain

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{5}(t)\sim{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com}(t),\quad\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{5}(t)\sim{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}(t).

(7.12)

Summing up (
7.8
), (
7.10
) and (
7.11
) yields directly that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{5}(t)+\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{5}(t)={\mathcal{L}}_{2}(t)+\sum_{i=1}^{5}K_{i}(t).

(7.13)

Here the linear term
{\mathcal{L}}_{2}
is defined by

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{2}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left\{-\frac{k^{2}\partial_{t}p}{p^{3}}|\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}|^{2}+\left(\frac{3}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}+\delta_{2}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}-2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}\right)|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}\right.

(7.14)

\displaystyle+\left[\delta_{2}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\left(\nu p^{\frac{1}{2}}+2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right)+\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\nu p\right)+\mu\frac{k^{2}\partial_{t}p}{p^{\frac{5}{2}}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\delta_{2}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(2\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+2\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}-\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}\right)\right]Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)

\displaystyle\left.+2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\left(\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}+\delta_{2}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\right)Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)-2\frac{k^{2}}{p}Re\left(\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right\}\,d\xi

\displaystyle+\delta_{2}\mu\sum_{s=1}^{3}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left\{|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{0}|^{2}+\nu|\xi|Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{0}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{0}\right)\right\}\,d\xi,

and the nonlinear terms
K_{i}(i=1,\cdots,5)
are defined by

\displaystyle K_{1}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{N}}_{1,s}^{k}\right)\,d\xi,

\displaystyle K_{2}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\,d\xi,

\displaystyle K_{3}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle-\delta_{2}\mu\sum_{s=1}^{3}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left[Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,1,s}^{0}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{0}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,1,s}^{0}\bar{\hat{N}}_{1,s}^{0}\right)\right]\,d\xi,

\displaystyle K_{4}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle-\sum_{s=0}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\left[Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{N}}_{1,s}^{k}\right)\right]\,d\xi,

\displaystyle K_{5}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle-\delta_{2}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\sum_{s=0}^{3}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left[Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{N}}_{1,s}^{k}\right)\right]\,d\xi.

We now estimate each term on the right-hand side of (
7.13
); the following lemma addresses the linear term
{\mathcal{L}}_{2}
.

Lemma 7.1
.

It holds that

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{2}(t)\leq\frac{3}{4}\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{5}(t)+C\mu^{-\frac{4}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\frac{C}{A}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}(t).

(7.15)

Proof.

We first estimate all the linear terms for
k\not=0
on the right-hand side of (
7.13
).
In view of (
3.9
) and (
5.42
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\frac{3}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}\right|\lesssim|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}\lesssim\varphi^{\frac{1}{2}}|p^{\frac{1}{2}}\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}\lesssim\mu^{-\frac{1}{3}}|p^{\frac{1}{2}}\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}.

(7.16)

The lift-up term in
D
is controlled by the dissipation of the lower-order velocity derivatives. Exploiting (
5.42
), we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\frac{k^{2}\partial_{t}p}{p^{3}}|\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}|^{2}\right|\lesssim\frac{k^{2}}{p}|\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}|^{2}.

(7.17)

It is not difficult to check that

\displaystyle\left|\delta_{2}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\nu p^{\frac{1}{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right|\lesssim\delta_{2}\mu^{\frac{4}{3}}\left(|\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}|^{2}+p|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}\right),

(7.18)

\displaystyle\left|\frac{1}{2}\nu\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right|\lesssim\nu|k|\left|Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right|\lesssim\widetilde{\epsilon}\nu p|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}+C_{\widetilde{\epsilon}}\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}|\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}|^{2}.

(7.19)

Again thanks to (
3.9
) and (
5.42
), we get

\displaystyle\left|\left(\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}+2\delta_{2}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}\right)Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right|

(7.20)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(1+2\delta_{2}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}})\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}\right)\left|Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}(1+2\delta_{2}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}})\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}\right)\left(|\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}\right).

In light of (
3.9
) and (
5.42
), we achieve that

\displaystyle\left|\left(2\delta_{2}\mu^{\frac{4}{3}}\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\mu\frac{k^{2}\partial_{t}p}{p^{\frac{5}{2}}}+\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}-\delta_{2}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right)Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right|\lesssim\frac{k^{2}}{p}\left(|\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}\right),

(7.21)

\displaystyle\left|2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\left(\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}+\delta_{2}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\right)Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right|\lesssim\frac{k^{2}}{p}\left(|\hat{N}_{1,s}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{W}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}\right),

(7.22)

\displaystyle\left|2\frac{k^{2}}{p}Re\left(\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{0,s}^{k}\right)\right|\lesssim\frac{k^{2}}{p}\left(|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{W}_{0,s}^{k}|^{2}\right).

(7.23)

For
k=0
, a direct computation yields immediately that

\displaystyle\left|\delta_{1}\mu\nu|\xi|Re\left(\hat{N}_{1,s}^{0}\bar{\hat{D}}_{0,s}^{0}\right)\right|\lesssim\delta_{1}\mu^{2}\left(|\hat{N}_{1,s}^{0}|^{2}+|\xi|^{2}|\hat{D}_{0,s}^{0}|^{2}\right).

(7.24)

Substituting (
7.16
)–(
7.24
) into (
7.14
), invoking (
3.1
), and then fixing
A
(defined in (
3.1
)) sufficiently large while taking
\delta_{2}
and
\widetilde{\epsilon}
sufficiently small, we immediately obtain (
7.15
). ∎

The nonlinear terms
K_{i}(i=1,\cdots,5)
will be dealt with in the following lemma.

Lemma 7.2
.

Under the assumptions of Proposition
7.1
, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{5}\int_{0}^{t}K_{i}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{1,2}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{5}{6}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

(7.25)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Proof.

For the first term
K_{1}
, proceeding as in the estimate of
I_{1}
in Lemma
5.2
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}\int_{0}^{t}K_{1}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{5}{6}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

(7.26)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}+\mu^{\frac{7}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Recalling (
2.12
), we get

\displaystyle K_{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\mathcal{R}_{2}\nabla^{s}\left(\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}D\right)D_{0,s}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|+\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\mathcal{R}_{2}\nabla^{s}\widetilde{{\mathscr{F}}}_{21}D_{0,s}\,dx_{1}dy_{1}\right|\triangleq\sum_{i=1}^{2}K_{2i},

(7.27)

where
\widetilde{{\mathscr{F}}}_{21}
is defined in (
2.14
). Following the same lines as in the estimate of
I_{1}
in Lemma
5.2
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}\int_{0}^{t}K_{21}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{5}{6}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

(7.28)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

The estimate proceeds in essentially the same manner as that for
J_{5}
in the proof of Proposition
6.3
, yielding

\displaystyle\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}\int_{0}^{t}K_{22}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{1,2}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{5}{6}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

(7.29)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

The combination of estimates (
7.27
)–(
7.29
) immediately yields

\displaystyle\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}\int_{0}^{t}K_{2}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{1,2}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{5}{6}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

(7.30)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

For
K_{3}
, arguing as in the proof of (
5.54
) in Lemma
5.4
, we deduce that

\displaystyle\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}\int_{0}^{t}K_{3}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{1,2}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{5}{6}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

(7.31)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

For
K_{4}
, we divide it into the following three terms:

\displaystyle K_{4}\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}\nabla^{s}\left(\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}N\right){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}D_{0,s}+{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\nabla^{s}\left(\bm{V}\cdot\widetilde{\nabla}D\right){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}_{3}N_{1,s}\right)\,dxdy\right|

(7.32)

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\mathcal{R}_{3}\nabla^{s}\left(ND\right){\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}D_{0,s}\,dxdy\right|+\sum_{s=0}^{3}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{T}}\times{\mathbb{R}}}\left({\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\nabla^{s}\widetilde{{\mathscr{F}}}_{21}{\mathbb{P}}_{\not=}\widetilde{\Delta}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{R}_{3}N_{1,s}\right)\,dxdy\right|,

where
\widetilde{{\mathscr{F}}}_{21}
is defined in (
2.14
).
Proceeding exactly as in the proofs of (
5.104
) and (
5.105
) in Lemma
5.5
and of (
6.50
) in Proposition
6.3
, we deduce from (
7.32
) that

\displaystyle\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}\int_{0}^{t}K_{4}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-\frac{2}{3}}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{5}{6}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

(7.33)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

It then follows by the same argument used to establish (
7.33
) that

\displaystyle\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}\int_{0}^{t}K_{5}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{5}{6}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

(7.34)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Collecting (
7.26
), (
7.30
), (
7.31
), (
7.33
) and (
7.34
) together, we obtain (
7.25
).
∎

With Lemmas
7.1
-
7.2
at hand, we can complete the proof of Proposition
7.1
.

Proof of Proposition
7.1
.

Integrating (
7.13
) with respect to t and then substituting (
7.15
) and (
7.25
) into the resulting inequality, we conclude that (
7.1
) holds. ∎

8
Mixed type energy estimates

Because the operator
\widetilde{\nabla}
differs from
\nabla
whenever
k\not=0
, cross-terms that couple “good” and “bad” derivatives of the density and velocity are no longer controllable. Consequently, even though the estimates for
{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{com,l}
,
{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{in}
and
{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{com}
with
j=0
were already obtained in Propositions
5.1
–
7.1
, we must still derive, within the
H^{s}
-framework with
1\leq s\leq 3
, analogous
a priori
energy bounds for solutions possessing
(s-j)
good derivatives and
j
bad derivatives, where
j
ranges from 1 to
s
.

8.1
Cross terms of compressible part

In this subsection, we establish the following proposition, which provides the
a priori
energy estimate for the cross-terms that couple the “good” and “bad” lower–order derivatives of the compressible part of the solution.

Proposition 8.1
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
2.2
and (
5.1
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{com,l}(t)+\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\int_{0}^{t}{\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{com,l}(\tau)\,d\tau

(8.1)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{com,l}(0)+\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2(j-1)}{3}}\int_{0}^{t}{\mathscr{D}}_{j-1,3-(j-1)}^{com,l}(\tau)\,d\tau+\frac{1}{A}\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\int_{0}^{t}{\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{in}(\tau)\,d\tau

\displaystyle+\mu^{-1}\sum_{j=0}^{3}\mu^{\frac{j}{3}}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{5}{6}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{com}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{in}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau,

where
c_{j}(j=1,2,3)
are some positive constant that will be determined later.

We derive from (
4
) that

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}=

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\frac{1}{4}\frac{\partial_{t}\varphi}{\varphi}\right)\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}+\frac{j}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}-p^{\frac{1}{2}}\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}+\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,j,s-j}^{k},

(8.2)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}=

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\frac{1}{4}\frac{\partial_{t}\varphi}{\varphi}+\nu p\right)\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}+\frac{j+1}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}

(8.3)

\displaystyle+\left(p^{\frac{1}{2}}+2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right)\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}-2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}-2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\varphi^{-\frac{1}{4}}\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}+\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,j,s-j}^{k},

where nonlinear terms are be determined explicitly as

\displaystyle\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,j,s-j}^{k}\triangleq

\displaystyle m_{1}^{-1}m_{2}^{-1}\varphi^{-\frac{1}{4}}<k,\xi>^{s-j}p^{\frac{j}{2}}\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1}^{k},

(8.4)

\displaystyle\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,j,s-j}^{k}\triangleq

\displaystyle m_{1}^{-1}m_{2}^{-1}\varphi^{-\frac{1}{4}}<k,\xi>^{s-j}p^{\frac{j-1}{2}}\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2}^{k}.

(8.5)

It follows from (
8.2
) and (
8.3
) that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}|(\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k},\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{j,s-j}^{k})|^{2}\,d\xi

(8.6)

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\frac{1}{4}\frac{\partial_{t}\varphi}{\varphi}\right)|\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\,d\xi

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\frac{1}{4}\frac{\partial_{t}\varphi}{\varphi}+\nu p\right)|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\,d\xi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left\{\frac{j}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}|\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+\frac{j+1}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.\qquad-2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}-2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\varphi^{-\frac{1}{4}}Re\left(\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right\}\,d\xi

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left\{Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{N}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right\}\,d\xi,

where
c_{j}(j=1,2,3)
are some positive constant that will be determined later.
We now establish the dissipation estimate for the density:

\displaystyle-\frac{d}{dt}\left[p^{-\frac{1}{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right]+\left(1+2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{2}}\right)|\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}

(8.7)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(2\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+2\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}\varphi}{\varphi}-j\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}+2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}+\nu p\right)Re\left(\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)

\displaystyle+2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)-\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left[Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{N}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right].

We observe that the dissipation estimates for the density differ between the zero and the non-zero frequencies.
For
k\not=0
, we multiply (
8.7
) by
\delta_{5}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}
, then integrate over
\xi
in
{\mathbb{R}}
, and sum over
k
in
{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}
to obtain

\displaystyle-\delta_{5}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}p^{-\frac{1}{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\,d\xi

(8.8)

\displaystyle+\delta_{5}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left(1+2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{2}}\right)|\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\,d\xi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{5}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left\{|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(2\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+2\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}\varphi}{\varphi}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.\left.-j\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}+2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}+\nu p\right)Re\left(\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)+2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left[Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{N}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right]\right\}\,d\xi.

While for
k=0
, we multiply (
8.7
) by
\delta_{5}\mu
, and integrate over
\xi
in
{\mathbb{R}}
, to obtain

\displaystyle-\delta_{5}\mu\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{s=2}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s-1}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}p^{-\frac{1}{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{0}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{j,s-j}^{0}\right)\,d\xi+\delta_{5}\mu\sum_{s=2}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s-1}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}|\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{0}|^{2}\,d\xi

(8.9)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{5}\mu\sum_{s=2}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s-1}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\Big\{|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{j,s-j}^{0}|^{2}+\nu p^{\frac{1}{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{0}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{j,s-j}^{0}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left[Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,j,s-j}^{0}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{j,s-j}^{0}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,j,s-j}^{0}\bar{\hat{N}}_{j,s-j}^{0}\right)\right]\Big\}\,d\xi.

We define

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{6}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left[|\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\right]\,d\xi

\displaystyle-2\delta_{5}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}p^{-\frac{1}{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\,d\xi

\displaystyle-2\delta_{5}\mu\sum_{s=2}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s-1}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}p^{-\frac{1}{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{0}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{j,s-j}^{0}\right)\,d\xi,

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{6}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\frac{1}{4}\frac{\partial_{t}\varphi}{\varphi}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\delta_{5}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\left(1+2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{2}}\right)\right)|\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\,d\xi+\delta_{5}\mu\sum_{s=2}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s-1}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}|\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{0}|^{2}\,d\xi

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\frac{1}{4}\frac{\partial_{t}\varphi}{\varphi}+\nu p\right)|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\,d\xi.

By some direct calculations, we obtain

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{6}(t)\sim\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{com}(t),\quad\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{6}(t)\sim\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{com}(t).

(8.10)

Combining (
8.6
), (
8.8
) and (
8.9
), we immediately obtain

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{6}(t)+\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{6}(t)={\mathcal{L}}_{3}(t)+\sum_{i=1}^{3}L_{i}.

(8.11)

Here the linear term
{\mathcal{L}}_{3}
and the nonlinear terms
L_{i}(i=1,2,3)
are defined by

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{3}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left\{\frac{j}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}|\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+\left(\frac{j+1}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}+\delta_{5}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}-2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}\right)|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\right.

(8.12)

\displaystyle+\left[\delta_{5}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\left(\nu p^{\frac{1}{2}}+2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right)+2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\delta_{5}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(2\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+2\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\frac{1}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}\varphi}{\varphi}-j\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\left.\times Re\left(\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)+2\delta_{5}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{k^{2}}{p^{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)-2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}Re\left(\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right\}\,d\xi

\displaystyle+\delta_{5}\mu\sum_{s=2}^{3}\sum_{j=2}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left\{|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{j,s-j}^{0}|^{2}+\nu p^{\frac{1}{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{0}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{j,s-j}^{0}\right)\right\}\,d\xi,

\displaystyle L_{1}\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left\{Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{N}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right\}\,d\xi,

\displaystyle L_{2}\triangleq

\displaystyle-\delta_{5}\mu\sum_{s=2}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s-1}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left[Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,j,s-j}^{0}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{j,s-j}^{0}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,j,s-j}^{0}\bar{\hat{N}}_{j,s-j}^{0}\right)\right]\,d\xi,

\displaystyle L_{3}\triangleq

\displaystyle-\delta_{5}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left[Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{N}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right]\,d\xi.

We now begin to deal with all the terms on the right-hand side of (
8.11
).
Firstly, we give the following lemma to deal with the linear term
{\mathcal{L}}_{3}
.

Lemma 8.1
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{3}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}{\mathcal{L}}_{3}(t)\leq\frac{1}{2}\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{6}(t)+C\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2(j-1)}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{j-1,3-(j-1)}^{com,l}(t)+\frac{C}{A}\sum_{j=1}^{3}{\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{in}(t).

(8.13)

Proof.

We begin by estimating every linear term in (
8.12
) for
k\not=0
.
The lift-up term containing the
j
-order bad derivative in the right-hand side of (
8.12
) can be controlled by the enhanced dissipation term involving the
j
-order and
(j-1)
-order bad derivative. Indeed, applying (
5.42
) together with Young’s inequality yields

\displaystyle\left|\frac{j}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}|\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+\frac{j+1}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\right|

(8.14)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{|k|}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(|\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\widetilde{\epsilon}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\left(|\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\right)+C_{\widetilde{\epsilon}}\mu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\left(|\hat{N}_{j-1,s-(j-1)}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{j-1,s-(j-1)}^{k}|^{2}\right).

The remaining terms in (
8.12
) can be estimated in the same way as in the proof of (
5.41
).
By Young’s inequality, we get

\displaystyle\left|2\delta_{5}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\frac{1}{4}\frac{\partial_{t}\varphi}{\varphi}\right)Re\left(\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right|

(8.15)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta_{5}\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\frac{1}{4}\frac{\partial_{t}\varphi}{\varphi}\right)\left(|\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\right).

After some direct calculations, we infer that

\displaystyle\left|\delta_{5}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\nu p^{\frac{1}{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right|\lesssim\delta_{5}\mu^{\frac{4}{3}}\left(|\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+p|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\right),

(8.16)

\displaystyle\left|2\delta_{5}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{k^{2}}{p^{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)-2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}Re\left(\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right|

(8.17)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{k^{2}}{p}\left(|\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\right).

By (
5.42
) again, we find that

\displaystyle\left|\left[2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\delta_{5}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}-j\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}\right)\right]Re\left(\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right|\lesssim\frac{k^{2}}{p}\left(|\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\right).

(8.18)

While for
k=0
, the linear terms on the right-hand side of (
8.11
) can be controlled by

\displaystyle\left|\delta_{5}\mu\nu|\xi|Re\left(\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{0}\bar{\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}}_{j,s-j}^{0}\right)\right|\lesssim\delta_{5}\mu^{2}\left(|\hat{N}_{j,s-j}^{0}|^{2}+|\xi|^{2}|\hat{{\mathcal{U}}}_{j,s-j}^{0}|^{2}\right).

(8.19)

Collecting all estimates (
8.14
)-(
8.19
) together, and then choosing
A
defined in (
3.1
) suitably large,
\widetilde{\epsilon}
and
\delta_{5}
suitably small, we can deduce (
8.13
).
∎

With the help of Lemma
8.1
, we can prove Proposition
8.1
.

Proof of Proposition
8.1
.

The nonlinear terms
L_{i}(i=1,2,3)
can be handled by the same arguments employed in the proof of Proposition
5.1
.
Indeed, because the energy
\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{6}
already incorporates at least one derivative of the solution, no separate estimate of the zero-mode in
L^{2}
is required. Consequently, the reasoning presented in Proposition
5.1
yields

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}L_{i}\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}\sum_{j=0}^{3}\mu^{\frac{j}{3}}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{5}{6}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{com}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{in}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Inserting the preceding estimate together with (
8.13
) into (
8.11
) and invoking (
3.1
) and (
8.10
), we arrive at (
8.1
). ∎

8.2
Cross-terms of incompressible part

Our goal in this subsection is to derive the
a priori
energy estimate for the cross-terms that couple the good and bad derivatives of the vorticity.

Proposition 8.2
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
2.2
and (
5.1
), we have

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{in}(t)+\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\int_{0}^{t}{\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{in}(\tau)\,d\tau

(8.20)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{in}(0)+\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2(j-1)}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{j-1,3-(j-1)}^{in}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}\sum_{j=0}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\int_{0}^{t}{\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{com}(\tau)\,d\tau

\displaystyle+\mu^{-1}\sum_{j=0}^{3}\mu^{\frac{j}{3}}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{5}{6}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{com}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{in}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Recalling
\eqref{eq_n-d-w-2}_{3}
once more, we obtain

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}=

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\mu p\right)\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}+\frac{j}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}-2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}

(8.21)

\displaystyle+\mu(\mu+\mu^{\prime})p\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}-\mu\left(\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}-2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}\right)\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}+2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}+\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{3,j+1,s-j}^{k},

where nonlinear term is defined by

\displaystyle\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{3,j+1,s-j}^{k}\triangleq

\displaystyle m_{1}^{-1}m_{2}^{-1}<k,\xi>^{s-j}p^{\frac{j}{2}}\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{3}^{k}.

(8.22)

Proof.

We deduce from (
8.21
) that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}|\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\mu p\right)|\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}

(8.23)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{j}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}|\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+\mu(\mu+\mu^{\prime})pRe\left(\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)-2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)

\displaystyle-\mu\left(\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}-2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}\right)Re\left(\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)+2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}|\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{3,j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right).

The lift-up term with
j
-order bad derivative on the right-hand side of (
8.23
) can be controlled by the enhanced dissipation term with
j
-order and
(j-1)
-order bad derivative. In fact, we have

\displaystyle\left|\frac{j}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}|\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\right|\lesssim\frac{|k|}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}|\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\lesssim\widetilde{\epsilon}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}|\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+C_{\widetilde{\epsilon}}\mu^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\hat{W}_{j-1,s-(j-1)}^{k}|^{2}.

(8.24)

Then by the same procedure as in (
6.35
)-(
6.37
), it is easy to verify that the other linear terms on the right-hand side of (
8.23
) can be computed as follows

\displaystyle\left|\mu(\mu+\mu^{\prime})pRe\left(\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right|\lesssim\widetilde{\epsilon}\mu p|\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+C_{\widetilde{\epsilon}}\mu^{3}p|\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2},

\displaystyle\left|2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right|\lesssim\widetilde{\epsilon}\frac{k^{2}}{p}|\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+C_{\widetilde{\epsilon}}\mu^{2}\frac{k^{2}}{p}|\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}|^{2},

\displaystyle\left|\mu\left(\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}-2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}\right)Re\left(\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right|\lesssim\widetilde{\epsilon}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}|\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+C_{\widetilde{\epsilon}}\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}\frac{k^{2}}{p}|\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}.

Plugging all the estimates above and (
8.24
) into (
8.23
), then choosing
A
defined in (
3.1
) suitably large and
\widetilde{\epsilon}
suitably small, we end up with

\displaystyle\frac{d}{dt}\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{7}(t)+\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{7}(t)\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left\{\mu^{-\frac{1}{3}}|\hat{W}_{j-1,s-(j-1)}^{k}|^{2}+\mu^{2}\frac{k^{2}}{p}|\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\right.

(8.25)

\displaystyle\left.+\mu^{3}p|\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}\frac{k^{2}}{p}|\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{3,j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right\}\,d\xi

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2(j-1)}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{j-1,3-(j-1)}^{in}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{com}

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{3,j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\,d\xi,

where
\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{7}
and
\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{7}
are defined by

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{7}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}|\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\,d\xi

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{7}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\mu p\right)|\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\,d\xi.

It is not difficult to check that

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{7}(t)\sim\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}{\mathcal{E}}_{j,s-j}^{in}(t),\quad\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{7}(t)\sim\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{j,s-j}^{in}(t).

(8.26)

The nonlinear term is controlled by an argument parallel to the proof of Proposition
6.1
.
Because the energy
\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{7}
already contains at least one derivative of the solution, no separate
L^{2}
estimate for the zero mode is required; consequently, the proof is simpler and is therefore omitted. One obtains

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\left|\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{3,j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\,d\xi\right|\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}\sum_{j=0}^{3}\mu^{\frac{j}{3}}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{5}{6}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{com}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{in}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau,

which, together with (
8.25
) and (
8.26
), yields (
8.20
) immediately.
∎

8.3
Cross-terms of compressible part with higher-order derivatives

In this section we establish the
a-priori
energy estimate for the cross terms that couple the good and bad higher-order derivatives arising from the compressible component of the solution. A precise statement is given in the following proposition.

Proposition 8.3
.

Under the assumptions of Theorem
2.2
and (
5.1
), we have

\displaystyle\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{com}(t)+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\int_{0}^{t}{\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{com}(\tau)\,d\tau

(8.27)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{com}(0)+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2(j-1)}{3}}\int_{0}^{t}{\mathscr{D}}_{j-1,3-(j-1)}^{com}(\tau)\,d\tau

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{A}\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\int_{0}^{t}{\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{in}(\tau)\,d\tau+\mu^{-1}\sum_{j=0}^{3}\mu^{\frac{j}{3}}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\mu^{\frac{5}{6}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{com}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{in}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau,

where
A
is defined in (
3.1
) and will be determined later.

Recalling
\eqref{eq_n-d-w-2}_{1}
and
\eqref{eq_n-d-w-2}_{2}
once more, we obtain

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}=

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}\right)\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}+\frac{j+1}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}-p^{\frac{1}{2}}\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}+\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,j+1,s-j}^{k},

(8.28)

\displaystyle\partial_{t}\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}=

\displaystyle-\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\nu p\right)\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}+\frac{j+2}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}+\left(p^{\frac{1}{2}}+2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right)\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}

(8.29)

\displaystyle-2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}-2\frac{k^{2}}{p}\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}+\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,j+1,s-j}^{k},

where nonlinear terms are defined by

\displaystyle\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,j+1,s-j}^{k}\triangleq

\displaystyle m_{1}^{-1}m_{2}^{-1}<k,\xi>^{s-j}p^{\frac{j+1}{2}}\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1}^{k},

(8.30)

\displaystyle\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,j+1,s-j}^{k}\triangleq

\displaystyle m_{1}^{-1}m_{2}^{-1}<k,\xi>^{s-j}p^{\frac{j}{2}}\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2}^{k}.

(8.31)

According to (
8.28
) and (
8.29
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left[|\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\right]\,d\xi

(8.32)

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}\right)|\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\,d\xi

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\nu p\right)|\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\,d\xi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left\{\frac{j+1}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}|\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+\frac{j+2}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}|\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)-2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}|\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}-2\frac{k^{2}}{p}Re\left(\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right\}\,d\xi

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left\{Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{N}}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right\}\,d\xi.

In order to obtain the dissipation for
\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}
, we compute that

\displaystyle-\frac{d}{dt}\left[p^{-\frac{1}{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right]+\left(1+2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{2}}\right)|\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}|^{2}

(8.33)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle|\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(2\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+2\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}-(j+1)\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}+2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}+\nu p\right)Re\left(\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)

\displaystyle+2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)-\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left[Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{N}}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}\right)\right].

Multiplying (
8.33
) by
\delta_{6}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}
, then summing over
k\in{\mathbb{Z}}
,
j
from
1
to
s
and over
s
from
1
to
3
, we achieve that

\displaystyle-\delta_{6}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left[p^{-\frac{1}{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right]\,d\xi

(8.34)

\displaystyle+\delta_{6}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left(1+2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{2}}\right)|\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\,d\xi

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{6}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left\{|\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(2\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+2\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}-(j+1)\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}\right.\right.

\displaystyle\left.+2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}+\nu p\right)Re\left(\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)+2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left[Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{N}}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}\right)\right]\right\}\,d\xi.

When
k=0
, we multiply (
8.33
) by
\delta_{6}\mu
, sum over

from

to

and over

from
