\displaystyle-\delta_{6}\mu\frac{d}{dt}\sum_{s=2}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s-1}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left[p^{-\frac{1}{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{0}\bar{\hat{D}}_{j,s-j}^{0}\right)\right]\,d\xi+\delta_{6}\mu\sum_{s=2}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s-1}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}|\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{0}|^{2}\,d\xi

(8.35)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\delta_{6}\mu\sum_{s=2}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s-1}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left\{|\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{0}|^{2}+\nu p^{\frac{1}{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{0}\bar{\hat{D}}_{j,s-j}^{0}\right)\right.

\displaystyle\left.-\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left[Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,j+1,s-j}^{0}\bar{\hat{D}}_{j,s-j}^{0}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,j+1,s-j}^{0}\bar{\hat{N}}_{j,s-j}^{0}\right)\right]\right\}\,d\xi.

We then define

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{8}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left(|\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\right)\,d\xi

(8.36)

\displaystyle-2\delta_{6}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left[p^{-\frac{1}{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right]\,d\xi

\displaystyle-2\delta_{6}\mu\sum_{s=2}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s-1}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}p^{-\frac{1}{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{0}\bar{\hat{D}}_{j,s-j}^{0}\right)\,d\xi,

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{8}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left[\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\delta_{6}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\left(1+2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{2}}\right)\right]|\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\,d\xi

(8.37)

\displaystyle+\delta_{6}\mu\sum_{s=2}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s-1}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}|\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{0}|^{2}\,d\xi

\displaystyle+\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}+\nu p\right)|\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\,d\xi.

A direct computation yields that

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{8}(t)\sim\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}{\mathcal{E}}_{j,s-j}^{com}(t),\quad\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{8}(t)\sim\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{j,s-j}^{com}(t).

(8.38)

Summing up (
8.32
), (
8.34
) and (
8.35
), we conclude

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\widetilde{{\mathcal{E}}}_{8}(t)+\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{8}(t)={\mathcal{L}}_{4}(t)+\sum_{i=1}^{3}M_{i}(t),

(8.39)

where the linear term
{\mathcal{L}}_{4}(t)
and the nonlinear terms
M_{i}(i=1,2,3)
are defined by

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{4}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left\{\frac{j+1}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}|\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+\left(\frac{j+2}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}+\delta_{6}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}-2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}\right)|\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\right.

(8.40)

\displaystyle+\left[2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\delta_{6}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\left(\nu p^{\frac{1}{2}}+2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}\right)+\delta_{6}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(2\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+2\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}-(j+1)\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\left.\times Re\left(\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)+2\delta_{6}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)-2\frac{k^{2}}{p}Re\left(\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right\}\,d\xi

\displaystyle+\delta_{6}\mu\sum_{s=2}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s-1}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left[|\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{0}|^{2}+\nu p^{\frac{1}{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{0}\bar{\hat{D}}_{j,s-j}^{0}\right)\right]\,d\xi,

\displaystyle M_{1}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\left[Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{N}}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right]\,d\xi,

\displaystyle M_{2}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle-\delta_{6}\mu\sum_{s=2}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s-1}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left[Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,j+1,s-j}^{0}\bar{\hat{D}}_{j,s-j}^{0}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,j+1,s-j}^{0}\bar{\hat{N}}_{j+1,s-j}^{0}\right)\right]\,d\xi,

\displaystyle M_{3}(t)\triangleq

\displaystyle-\delta_{6}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\sum_{s=1}^{3}\sum_{j=1}^{s}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\sum_{k\in{\mathbb{Z}}\backslash\{0\}}\int_{{\mathbb{R}}}\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left[Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{1,j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)+Re\left(\hat{{\mathscr{F}}}_{2,j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{N}}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}\right)\right]\,d\xi.

Lemma 8.2
.

It holds that

\displaystyle{\mathcal{L}}_{4}(t)\leq\frac{1}{4}\widetilde{{\mathscr{D}}}_{8}(t)+C\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2(j-1)}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{j-1,3-(j-1)}^{com}(t)+\frac{C}{A}\sum_{j=1}^{3}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{in}(t).

(8.41)

Proof.

We begin by treating all linear terms on the right-hand side of (
8.40
) for
k\not=0
.
The lift-up term involving the
j
-order bad derivative therein is then absorbed by the enhanced dissipation, which contains both
j
-order and
(j-1)
-order bad derivative.
Indeed, from (
5.42
) we deduce

\displaystyle\left|\frac{j+1}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}|\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+\frac{j+2}{2}\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}|\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\right|

(8.42)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{|k|}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(|\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\widetilde{\epsilon}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\left(|\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\right)+C_{\widetilde{\epsilon}}\mu^{-\frac{1}{3}}\left(|\hat{N}_{j,s-(j-1)}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{D}_{j-1,s-(j-1)}^{k}|^{2}\right).

Sine
p\geq 1
for
k\not=0
, we have

\displaystyle\left|2\delta_{6}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}\right)Re\left(\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right|

(8.43)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\delta_{6}\left(\frac{\partial_{t}m_{1}}{m_{1}}+\frac{\partial_{t}m_{2}}{m_{2}}\right)\left(|\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\right),

\displaystyle\left|\delta_{6}\mu^{\frac{4}{3}}p^{\frac{1}{2}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right|\lesssim\delta_{6}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\nu\left(|\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+p|\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\right),

(8.44)

and

\displaystyle\left|2\delta_{6}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}Re\left(\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{W}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)-2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}Re\left(\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right|

(8.45)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{k^{2}}{p}\left(|\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{W}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\right).

Applying (
5.42
) once more, we deduce that

\displaystyle\left|\left[2\frac{k^{2}}{p^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\delta_{6}\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}\frac{1}{p^{\frac{1}{2}}}\left(2\mu\frac{k^{2}}{p}-(j+1)\frac{\partial_{t}p}{p}\right)\right]Re\left(\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}\bar{\hat{D}}_{j,s-j}^{k}\right)\right|\lesssim\frac{k^{2}}{p}\left(|\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{k}|^{2}+|\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{k}|^{2}\right).

(8.46)

For
k=0
, it is not difficult to check that

\displaystyle\left|\delta_{6}\mu\nu|\xi|Re\left(\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{0}\bar{\hat{D}}_{j,s-j}^{0}\right)\right|\lesssim\delta_{6}\mu^{2}\left(|\hat{N}_{j+1,s-j}^{0}|^{2}+|\xi|^{2}|\hat{D}_{j,s-j}^{0}|^{2}\right).

(8.47)

Inserting all the preceding estimates into (
8.39
) and choosing
A
(defined in (
3.1
)) sufficiently large and

Inserting all the estimates above into (
8.39
), choosing
A
defined in (
3.1
) suitably large,
\widetilde{\epsilon}
and
\delta_{6}
suitably smalll, we immediately obtain (
8.41
).
∎

Proof of Propostion
8.3
.

It follows from the same argument used in the proof of Proposition
7.1
that

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{3}\int_{0}^{t}M_{i}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim

\displaystyle\mu^{-1}\sum_{j=0}^{3}\mu^{\frac{j}{3}}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{com}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{j+1,3-j}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{5}{6}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{j+1,3-j}^{com}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

(8.48)

\displaystyle\times\int_{0}^{t}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{com}+{\mathscr{D}}_{j+1,3-j}^{in}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{j+1,3-j}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Substituting (
8.41
) and (
8.48
) into (
8.39
), then applying (
8.38
), we obtain (
8.27
) directly.
∎

9
Proof of main result

In this section we prove Theorem
2.2
. The global existence theory for nonlinear PDEs is usually carried out in three steps: (1) construction of suitable approximate solutions;
(2) uniform
a priori
energy estimates for these approximations; (3) passage to the limit. Steps (1) and (3) are standard. For brevity we therefore focus on the
a priori
energy estimates for smooth solutions of (
2.2
) within the
H^{4}
framework. Once Proposition
4.1
is established, the theorem follows immediately by a continuity argument; the remaining details are omitted. We now proceed to the proof of Proposition
4.1
.

Proof of Proposition
4.1
.

Using the bootstrap argument, we first assume that the density bound (
5.1
) holds. Under this assumption we have already shown that (
5.9
), (
6.8
) and (
7.1
) remain valid for all time. Multiplying (
6.8
) by
\tilde{\delta}_{1}
and (
7.1
) by
\tilde{\delta}_{2}
, adding the resulting equality to (
5.9
) together, then taking
A
sufficiently large and
\tilde{\delta}_{1}
,
\tilde{\delta}_{2}
sufficiently small, we obtain

\displaystyle{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)+\tilde{\delta}_{1}{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)+\tilde{\delta}_{2}\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com}(t)+\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\tilde{\delta}_{1}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}+\tilde{\delta}_{2}\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau

(9.1)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle~{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(0)+\tilde{\delta}_{1}{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(0)+\tilde{\delta}_{2}\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com}(0)+\mu^{-1}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{1}{3}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{1,2}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{5}{6}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{0,3}^{com}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com,l}+\mu^{\frac{2}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{in}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{0,3}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Notice that
{\mathcal{E}}_{1,2}^{com,l}(t)
and
{\mathscr{D}}_{1,2}^{com,l}(t)
can not be controlled by the terms on the left-hand side of (
9.1
). Thus we must bound the cross terms involving both the good and bad derivatives of the density and velocity.
Multiplying (
8.20
) by
\tilde{\delta}_{1}
and (
8.27
) by
\tilde{\delta}_{1}
and
\tilde{\delta}_{2}
, then adding the resulting equality to (
8.1
), and then choosing
A
sufficiently large and
\tilde{\delta}_{1},\tilde{\delta}_{2}
sufficiently small, we obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\left({\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{com,l}(t)+\tilde{\delta}_{1}{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{in}(t)+\tilde{\delta}_{2}\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{com}(t)\right)

(9.2)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{com,l}+\tilde{\delta}_{1}{\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{in}+\tilde{\delta}_{2}\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\left({\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{com,l}(0)+\tilde{\delta}_{1}{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{in}(0)+\tilde{\delta}_{2}\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{com}(0)\right)

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=1}^{3}c_{j}\mu^{\frac{2(j-1)}{3}}\int_{0}^{t}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{j-1,3-(j-1)}^{com,l}+\tilde{\delta}_{1}{\mathscr{D}}_{j-1,3-(j-1)}^{in}+\tilde{\delta}_{2}\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{j-1,3-(j-1)}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau

\displaystyle+\mu^{-1}\sum_{j=0}^{3}\mu^{\frac{j}{3}}\left(|{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{com,l}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+|{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{in}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}+\mu^{\frac{5}{6}}|{\mathcal{E}}_{j,3-j}^{com}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad\times\int_{0}^{t}\mu^{\frac{2j}{3}}\left({\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{com,l}+{\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{in}+\mu^{\frac{5}{3}}{\mathscr{D}}_{j,3-j}^{com}\right)(\tau)\,d\tau.

Multiplying (
9.1
) by
c_{0}
, and adding the result inequality to (
9.2
), then choosing suitable
c_{j}
such that
c_{j}\leq C_{1}c_{j-1}
holds for any
j=1,\cdots,3
, finally using the
a priori
assumption (
4.12
), we then deduce that

\displaystyle\sup_{\tau\in[0,t]}{\mathcal{E}}(\tau)+\int_{0}^{t}{\mathscr{D}}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim{\mathcal{E}}(0)+|{\mathcal{E}}(t)|^{\frac{1}{2}}\int_{0}^{t}{\mathscr{D}}(\tau)\,d\tau\lesssim{\mathcal{E}}(0)+\epsilon_{0}^{3}\mu^{2},

(9.3)

where
{\mathcal{E}}(t)
and
{\mathscr{D}}(t)
are defined in (
4.13
) and (
4.14
), respectively. Then by Sobolev’s inequality, we get

\displaystyle\frac{3}{4}\leq N+1\leq\frac{3}{2},\quad\textrm{for all}\quad t\in[0,\infty).

This finishes the proof of Proposition
4.1
.
∎
